                                @click="addToCart"
                            >
                                <span class="icon-add-to-cart" aria-hidden="true"></span>


                            </button>

                            <button
                                v-if="! compact"
                                v-for="quickQuantity in quickQuantities"
                                :key="'quick-quantity-' + product.id + '-' + quickQuantity"
                                type="button"
                                class="grid-card-side-action secondary-button max-md:hidden shrink-0 px-3 py-2.5 max-md:px-2 max-md:py-2 text-sm font-medium disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-50 max-sm:rounded-xl"
                                style="align-self: stretch"
                                :disabled="! canAddQuickQty(quickQuantity)"
                                @click="addQuickQty(quickQuantity)"
                            >
                                +{{ quickQuantity }}
                            </button>

                                                            <span
                                v-if="! compact && quickQuantities.length === 0"
                                    class="grid-card-side-action grid-card-side-action-placeholder"
                                    aria-hidden="true"
                                ></span>
                                                    </div>

                        <v-button v-if="compact && maxQty <= 0" type="button" class="primary-button w-full max-w-full cursor-not-allowed p-2.5 text-sm font-medium opacity-50 pointer-events-none max-sm:rounded-xl max-sm:p-2" button-type="primary-button" :title="outOfStockText" disabled="disabled"></v-button>


                        <div v-if="! compact" class="flex w-full items-center gap-2">

                    <!-- Немає в наявності / ціна за запитом -->
                    <v-button v-if="maxQty <= 0 || isPriceOnRequest" type="button" class="primary-button opacity-50 cursor-not-allowed pointer-events-none w-full max-w-full p-2.5 text-sm font-medium max-sm:rounded-xl max-sm:p-2" button-type="primary-button" :title="isPriceOnRequest ? addToCartText : outOfStockText" disabled="disabled"></v-button>


                    <!-- Додати в кошик -->
                    <button
                        v-else
                        type="button"
                        class="secondary-button w-full max-w-full p-2.5 text-base font-medium max-sm:rounded-xl max-sm:p-2"
                        :class="isAddToCartDisabled ? 'opacity-50 cursor-not-allowed' : ''"
                        :disabled="isAddToCartDisabled"
                        :aria-disabled="isAddToCartDisabled ? 'true' : 'false'"
                        @click="addToCart"
                    >
                        Купити                    </button>


                        
                                                          
                                                <span
                            class="grid-card-side-action cursor-pointer p-2.5 text-2xl max-sm:hidden"
                            role="button"
                            aria-label="Додати до Списку Бажань"
                            tabindex="0"
                            :class="product.is_wishlist ? 'icon-heart-fill text-red-600' : 'icon-heart'"
                            @click="addToWishlist()"
                        ></span>
                                                
                        </div>

                    </div>


                        
                                                
                    </div>
                    </div>
            </div>

            <!-- List Card -->
            <div
                ref="listCard"
                class="list-product-card group relative flex w-full gap-5 overflow-visible rounded-md bg-white p-4 transition-all duration-300 1180:overflow-hidden max-sm:grid max-sm:grid-cols-1 max-sm:gap-3 max-sm:p-2.5"
                :class="{
                    'has-second-image': hasSecondImage,
                    'has-loaded-second-image': isHoverImageLoaded,
                }"
                :title="product.name"
                v-else
            >
                <!-- left image column -->
                <div class="list-product-card__image relative h-[258px] w-[250px] shrink-0 overflow-visible rounded 1180:overflow-hidden max-md:h-[220px] max-md:w-[220px] max-sm:h-auto max-sm:w-full">
                    

                    <a
                        :href="`https://yumi.market/${product.url_key}`"
                        class="relative block w-full swipe-previewable"
                        :aria-label="product.name + ' '"
                        @click="onImageClick"
                    >

                   <!-- Mobile: scroll-snap -->
                    <div v-if="isMobile && ! disableImageSwipe" class="w-full overflow-visible">
                        <div
                            ref="mobileSnapList"
                            :key="product.id + '-mobile-track-list'"
                            class="mobile-snap-track flex aspect-square w-full overflow-x-auto overflow-y-hidden rounded snap-x snap-mandatory scrollbar-hide"
                            @scroll.passive="onMobileSnapScroll('mobileSnapList')"
                            @touchstart.passive="onImageTouchStart"
                            @touchmove.passive="onImageTouchMove"
                        >
                        <!-- slide 1: base -->
                        <div class="mobile-snap-slide h-full min-w-full basis-full flex-none snap-start overflow-hidden bg-white">
                            <v-shimmer-image class="after:content-[' '] relative bg-white after:block after:pb-[calc(100%)]" :src="baseImageUrl" :lazy="!priority" :fetch-priority="priority ? 'high' : 'auto'" :key="product.id + '-base-list'" :index="product.id + '-base-list'" width="440" height="440" :alt="product.name">
    <div class="shimmer after:content-[' '] relative bg-white after:block after:pb-[calc(100%)]" :src="baseImageUrl" :lazy="!priority" :fetch-priority="priority ? 'high' : 'auto'" :key="product.id + '-base-list'" :index="product.id + '-base-list'" width="440" height="440" :alt="product.name"></div>
</v-shimmer-image>


                            <div class="absolute inset-0 pointer-events-none z-10 p-2">
                                <div class="absolute left-0 md:left-4 z-10 top-0 sm:top-2 flex flex-col gap-1 items-start">
                                    <template v-for="(item, i) in (product.placement_top_left || [])" :key="'list-mobile-ptl-' + i">
                                        <div v-if="item && item.value" class="flex items-center gap-1 rounded px-0 py-1 text-sm sm:text-sm text-gray-900 s-hadow">
                                            <template v-if="item.icon">
                                                <span v-if="isColor(item.icon)" class="h-3 w-3 rounded-full border" :style="{ backgroundColor: item.icon }"></span>
                                                <img v-else class="h-4 w-4 object-contain" :src="iconUrl(item.icon)" alt="" />
                                            </template>

                                            <span class="font-medium">{{ item.value }}</span>
                                        </div>
                                    </template>
                                </div>

                                <div class="absolute right-0 md:right-4 z-10 top-0 sm:top-2 flex flex-col gap-1 items-end">
                                    <template v-for="(item, i) in (product.placement_top_right || [])" :key="'list-mobile-ptr-' + i">
                                        <div v-if="item && item.value" class="flex items-center gap-1 rounded px-0 py-1 text-sm sm:text-sm text-gray-900 s-hadow">
                                            <template v-if="item.icon">
                                                <span v-if="isColor(item.icon)" class="h-3 w-3 rounded-full border" :style="{ backgroundColor: item.icon }"></span>
                                                <img v-else class="h-4 w-4 object-contain" :src="iconUrl(item.icon)" alt="" />
                                            </template>

                                            <span class="font-medium">{{ item.value }}</span>
                                        </div>
                                    </template>
                                </div>

                                <div class="absolute left-0 z-10 bottom-0 flex flex-col gap-1 items-start">
                                    <template v-for="(item, i) in (product.placement_bottom_left || [])" :key="'list-mobile-pbl-' + i">
                                        <div v-if="item && item.value" class="flex items-center gap-1 rounded px-0 py-1 text-sm sm:text-sm text-gray-900 s-hadow">
                                            <template v-if="item.icon">
                                                <span v-if="isColor(item.icon)" class="h-3 w-3 rounded-full border" :style="{ backgroundColor: item.icon }"></span>
                                                <img v-else class="h-4 w-4 object-contain" :src="iconUrl(item.icon)" alt="" />
                                            </template>

                                            <span class="font-medium">{{ item.value }}</span>
                                        </div>
                                    </template>
                                </div>

                                <div class="absolute right-0 md:right-4 z-10 bottom-0 flex flex-col gap-1 items-end">
                                    <template v-for="(item, i) in (product.placement_bottom_right || [])" :key="'list-mobile-pbr-' + i">
                                        <div v-if="item && item.value" class="flex items-center gap-1 rounded px-0 py-1 text-sm sm:text-sm text-gray-900 s-hadow">
                                            <template v-if="item.icon">
                                                <span v-if="isColor(item.icon)" class="h-3 w-3 rounded-full border" :style="{ backgroundColor: item.icon }"></span>
                                                <img v-else class="h-4 w-4 object-contain" :src="iconUrl(item.icon)" alt="" />
                                            </template>

                                            <span class="font-medium">{{ item.value }}</span>
                                        </div>
                                    </template>
                                </div>
                            </div>
                        </div>

                        <!-- slide 2: hover -->
                        <div v-if="hasSecondImage" class="mobile-snap-slide h-full min-w-full basis-full flex-none snap-start overflow-hidden bg-white">
                        <img
    v-if="hasSecondImage"
    :key="product.id + '-hover-list-mobile'"
    :src="hoverImageUrl"
    class="h-full w-full bg-white object-contain"
    width="440"
    height="440"
    loading="lazy"
    decoding="async"
    fetchpriority="low"
    alt=""
    aria-hidden="true"
    @load="isHoverImageLoaded = true"
@error="handleHoverImageError"
/>
                    </div>
                    </div>
            <div
            v-if="hasSecondImage"
            class="absolute -bottom-4 left-1/2 z-[1] -translate-x-1/2 pointer-events-none"
        >
            <div class="flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-full bg-white/70 px-0 py-1">
                <span
                    class="h-1.5 w-1.5 rounded-full"
                    :class="mobileSlideIndex === 0 ? 'bg-gray-900/70' : 'bg-gray-900/25'"
                ></span>

                <span
                    class="h-1.5 w-1.5 rounded-full"
                    :class="mobileSlideIndex === 1 ? 'bg-gray-900/70' : 'bg-gray-900/25'"
                ></span>
            </div>
        </div>
        </div>

                        <!-- Static image for carousels; desktop hover image otherwise -->
                        <div v-else class="image-hover-group relative">
            <v-shimmer-image class="pc-base-image after:content-[' '] relative bg-white transition-all duration-300 after:block after:pb-[calc(100%)] opacity-100" :class="''" :src="baseImageUrl" :lazy="!priority" :fetch-priority="priority ? 'high' : 'auto'" :key="product.id + '-desk-list'" :index="product.id + '-desk-list'" width="440" height="440" :alt="product.name">
    <div class="shimmer pc-base-image after:content-[' '] relative bg-white transition-all duration-300 after:block after:pb-[calc(100%)] opacity-100" :class="''" :src="baseImageUrl" :lazy="!priority" :fetch-priority="priority ? 'high' : 'auto'" :key="product.id + '-desk-list'" :index="product.id + '-desk-list'" width="440" height="440" :alt="product.name"></div>
</v-shimmer-image>


           <img
    v-if="hasSecondImage"
    :key="product.id + '-hover-desk'"
    :src="hoverImageUrl"
    class="pc-hover-image absolute inset-0 h-full w-full bg-white object-contain opacity-0 pointer-events-none transition-opacity duration-200 ease-out"
    width="440"
    height="440"
    loading="lazy"
    decoding="async"
    fetchpriority="low"
    alt=""
    aria-hidden="true"
    @load="isHoverImageLoaded = true"
@error="handleHoverImageError"
/>
        </div>

                    </a>

                    
                    <div class="action-items bg-black">
                        <div class="opacity-0 transition-all duration-300 group-hover:opacity-100 max-lg:opacity-100 max-sm:opacity-100">
                            
                                                        <span class="absolute top-6 flex h-8 w-8 cursor-pointer items-center justify-center rounded-full border border-zinc-200 bg-white text-xl ltr:right-0 rtl:left-2.5"
                                  role="button"
                                  aria-label="Додати до Списку Бажань"
                                  tabindex="0"
                                  :class="product.is_wishlist ? 'icon-heart-fill text-red-600' : 'icon-heart'"
                                  @click="addToWishlist()"></span>
                                                        
                            
                                                        
                        </div>
                    </div>
                </div>

                <!-- right info column -->
                <div class="flex min-w-0 flex-1 flex-col justify-between gap-4">
                    <div class="grid content-start gap-2.5">
                    <div class="flex flex-wrap items-start gap-1 max-sm:min-h-[20px]">
                        <p
                            class="top-1.5 inline-block w-max rounded-xl bg-red-700 px-2.5 py-1 text-sm text-white max-sm:px-2 max-sm:py-0.5 max-sm:text-xs ltr:left-1.5 max-sm:ltr:left-0 rtl:right-5 max-sm:rtl:right-0"
                            v-if="product.on_sale && Number(product?.special_discount ?? 0) > 0"
                        >-{{ product.special_discount }}%</p>

                        <p
                            v-for="badge in product.badges || []"
                            :key="'list-badge-' + badge.code"
                            class="top-1.5 inline-block w-max rounded-xl px-2.5 py-1 text-sm text-white max-sm:rounded-r-lg max-sm:px-2 max-sm:py-0.5 max-sm:text-xs ltr:left-1.5 max-sm:ltr:left-0 rtl:right-1.5 max-sm:rtl:right-0"
                            :style="{ backgroundColor: badge.background_color || '#6b7280' }"
                        >
                            {{ badge.label }}
                        </p>
                    </div>

                    <div class="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-x-4 gap-y-1 max-w-[360px] max-sm:flex-col max-sm:items-start">
                        <p
                            class="text-sm text-stone-800 ltr:left-1.5 max-sm:ltr:left-0 rtl:right-1.5 max-sm:rtl:right-0"
                            style="cursor: text; user-select: text; -webkit-user-select: text"
                            @pointerdown.stop
                        >
                              Арт: {{ product.sku }}
                        </p>

                        <span
                            v-if="!isMTO"
                            :class="['flex items-center rounded-xl   px-0 bg-opacity-70 font-medium text-sm sm:text-sm', qtyBadgeClass]"
                        >
                            <span
                                :class="[
                                    'inline-block h-1.5 w-1.5 mr-1 rounded-full',
                                    qty > 0 ? 'bg-lime-500' : 'bg-red-500'
                                ]"
                            ></span>
                            {{ qty }} шт                        </span>

                        <span v-else class="inline-block rounded-xl bg-amber-100 px-2.5 py-1 text-sm text-stone-800 max-sm:px-2 max-sm:py-0.5 max-sm:text-xs">
                            Під замовлення                        </span>

                    </div>

                    
                    <span
                        :aria-label="product.name"
                        role="link"
                        tabindex="0"
                       class="product-card-name max-1180:line-clamp-3 1180:line-clamp-2
                               break-words max-w-[360px]
                               text-base font-medium transition-colors hover:text-navyBlue
                               max-sm:text-sm max-sm:leading-5"
                        style="cursor: pointer; user-select: text; -webkit-user-select: text"
                        @pointerdown.stop="namePressStartedAt = Date.now()"
                        @click="openProductPage($event)"
                        @keydown.enter.prevent="openProductPage($event, true)"
                    >
                        {{ product.name }}
                    </span>
                    

                    
                    <div class="grid max-w-[360px] grid-cols-[1fr_auto] items-baseline gap-x-3 gap-y-0">

                        <span class="text-sm">За 1 шт</span>
          <div class="flex items-center gap-2.5  text-md font-semibold max-sm:text-sm max-sm:leading-6" v-html="product.price_html"></div>

                        <span v-if="false" class="hidden rounded-xl bg-amber-100 px-2.5 py-1 text-sm text-stone-800">
                          Під замовлення
                        </span>
                    </div>
                    

                    <div
                        v-if="product.extra_prices && product.extra_prices.length"
                        class="grid max-w-[360px] grid-cols-[1fr_auto] items-baseline gap-x-3 gap-y-0"
                    >
                        <template
                            v-for="price in product.extra_prices.slice(0, 3)"
                            :key="'extra-price-list-' + product.id + '-' + price.qty"
                        >
                            <span class="text-sm sm:text-sm">
                                {{ price.label }}
                            </span>

                            <span class="whitespace-nowrap text-right text-md font-semibold text-gray-900">
                                {{ price.formatted_price }}
                            </span>
                        </template>
                    </div>

                    
                    <div class="action-items mt-1.5 flex w-full max-w-[360px] items-center gap-2 max-sm:max-w-none">
                        <!-- quantity selector (list) -->
                        <v-quantity-changer :min-value="1" class="border border-gray-300 !w-40 shrink-0 justify-between rounded-xl px-3 py-2
                                   [&_input[type=number]]:!w-14 [&_input[type=number]]:!p-0" name="" v-if="maxQty >= 1" :value="selectedQty" :max-value="maxQty" @change="selectedQty = $event">
</v-quantity-changer>



                        <v-button class="secondary-button w-full max-w-full p-2.5 text-base font-medium max-sm:rounded-xl max-sm:p-2" button-type="primary-button" :title="(maxQty <= 0 || isPriceOnRequest) ? (isPriceOnRequest ? addToCartText : outOfStockText) : addToCartText" :loading="isAddingToCart" :disabled="isAddToCartDisabled" @click="addToCart()"></v-button>

                    </div>
                    

                </div>
            </div>
            </div>

        </script>

        <script type="module">
            const responsiveProductCards = new Set();

            // Set to false to restore the previous immediate CSS hover behaviour.
            const productCardHoverIntentEnabled = false;
            const productCardHoverIntentDelay = 120;
            const productCardScrollCooldown = 180;
            let productCardHoverBlockedUntil = 0;

            const productCardDesktopQuery = window.matchMedia('(min-width: 1180px)');
            const productCardFinePointerQuery = window.matchMedia('(hover: hover) and (pointer: fine)');

            const suspendProductCardHover = () => {
                if (! productCardHoverIntentEnabled) {
                    return;
                }

                productCardHoverBlockedUntil = performance.now() + productCardScrollCooldown;
                responsiveProductCards.forEach(component => component.deactivateGridCardHover());
            };

            window.addEventListener('wheel', suspendProductCardHover, { passive: true, capture: true });
            window.addEventListener('scroll', suspendProductCardHover, { passive: true, capture: true });

            const updateProductCardViewports = (event) => {
                responsiveProductCards.forEach(component => component.updateViewport(event.matches));
            };

            if (typeof productCardDesktopQuery.addEventListener === 'function') {
                productCardDesktopQuery.addEventListener('change', updateProductCardViewports);
            } else {
                productCardDesktopQuery.addListener(updateProductCardViewports);
            }

            app.component('v-product-card', {
                template: '#v-product-card-template',
                props: [
                    'mode',
                    'product',
                    'addToCartText',
                    'outOfStockText',
                    'priority',
                    'disableImageSwipe',
                    'compact',
                ],

                data() {
                    return {
                        isMobile: ! productCardDesktopQuery.matches,
                        mobileSlideIndex: 0,
                        isCustomer: '',
                        isAddingToCart: false,
                        selectedQty: 1,

                         // swipe preview state
                        isSwipePreviewActive: false,
                        touchStartX: 0,
                        touchStartY: 0,
                        touchDeltaX: 0,
                        touchDeltaY: 0,
                        didSwipeOnImage: false,
                        namePressStartedAt: 0,
                        actionItemsResizeObserver: null,
                        isHoverImageLoaded: false,
                        hoverImageFailed: false,
                        hoverIntentEnabled: productCardHoverIntentEnabled && productCardFinePointerQuery.matches,
                        hoverIntentTimer: null,
                        isHoverActive: false,
                    }
                },

                mounted() {
                    responsiveProductCards.add(this);
                    this.$nextTick(this.observeActionItemsHeight);
                },

                beforeUnmount() {
                    responsiveProductCards.delete(this);
                    this.actionItemsResizeObserver?.disconnect();
                    window.clearTimeout(this.hoverIntentTimer);
                },

                computed: {

                    isPriceOnRequest() {
                        const finalPrice = Number(this.product?.prices?.final?.price ?? 0);
                        const regularPrice = Number(this.product?.prices?.regular?.price ?? 0);

                        return finalPrice <= 0 && regularPrice <= 0;
                    },
                      baseImageUrl() {
                        return this.product?.base_image?.medium_image_url || '';
                    },

                    hoverImageUrl() {
                        return this.product?.hover_image?.medium_image_url || '';
                    },

                    hasSecondImage() {
                        return ! this.hoverImageFailed
                            && !! this.hoverImageUrl
                            && this.hoverImageUrl !== this.baseImageUrl;
                    },
                    hasCardBadges() {
                        return (this.product?.on_sale && Number(this.product?.special_discount ?? 0) > 0)
                            || (Array.isArray(this.product?.badges) && this.product.badges.length > 0);
                    },
                    old_price_to_show() {

                        const oldPrice = Number(this.product?.old_price ?? 0);
                        const currentPrice = Number(this.product?.prices.final.price ?? 0);
                        return oldPrice > currentPrice ? oldPrice : null;
                    },
                    isMTO() {
                       // console.log(this.product); console.log('+++++++++++++++++');
                        const v = this.product && this.product.made_to_order;
                        // нормалізуємо все до boolean
                        return v === true || v === 1 || v === '1';
                    },

                    qty() {

                         if(!this.product?.is_saleable)
                            return 0;

                        const val = this.product?.qty ?? this.product?.flat_qty ?? this.product?.product_qty ?? 0;
                        const n = Number(val);
                        return Number.isFinite(n) ? Math.max(0, Math.trunc(n)) : 0;
                    },

                    maxQty() {

                        if (this.isMTO) {
                            return 1000000;
                        }

                        return this.qty > 0 ? this.qty : 0;
                    },
                    quickQuantities() {
                        if (! Array.isArray(this.product?.extra_prices)) {
                            return [];
                        }

                        const quantities = this.product.extra_prices
                            .map(price => Number(price?.qty))
                            .filter(quantity => Number.isInteger(quantity) && quantity > 0);

                        return quantities.length ? [Math.min(...quantities)] : [];
                    },
                    qtyBadgeClass() {
                        return this.qty === 0 ? 'bg--red-100 text-red-600' : 'bg--gray-100 text-zinc-500';
                    },
                    isAddToCartDisabled() {
                        if (this.product?.type === 'grouped') {
                            return false; // завжди дозволено
                        }

                        if (this.isPriceOnRequest) return true;
                        if (!this.product?.is_saleable || this.isAddingToCart) return true;
                        if (!Number.isFinite(this.selectedQty) || this.selectedQty < 1) return true;
                        if (this.isMTO) return false;
                        if (this.qty === 0) return true;
                        if (this.selectedQty > this.maxQty) return true;
                        return false;
                    },
                },

                watch: {
                   hasSecondImage(hasSecondImage) {
                        if (! hasSecondImage) {
                            this.isSwipePreviewActive = false;
                            this.isHoverImageLoaded = false;
                        }
                    },
                   qty() {
                        this.normalizeQty();
                    },

                   hoverImageUrl() {
                        this.hoverImageFailed = false;
                        this.isHoverImageLoaded = false;
                        this.mobileSlideIndex = 0;
                   },

                   'product.id'() {
                        this.isSwipePreviewActive = false;
                        this.didSwipeOnImage = false;
                        this.mobileSlideIndex = 0;
                        this.isHoverImageLoaded = false;
                        this.hoverImageFailed = false;
                   },
                },

                methods: {
                    handleHoverImageError() {
                        this.hoverImageFailed = true;
                        this.isHoverImageLoaded = false;
                        this.mobileSlideIndex = 0;
                    },

                    updateViewport(isDesktop) {
                        const isMobile = ! isDesktop;

                        if (this.isMobile === isMobile) {
                            return;
                        }

                        this.isHoverImageLoaded = false;
                        this.mobileSlideIndex = 0;
                        this.isMobile = isMobile;

                        if (isMobile) {
                            this.deactivateGridCardHover();
                        }
                    },

                    handleGridCardMouseEnter(event) {
                        if (this.compact) {
                            return;
                        }

                        if (! this.hoverIntentEnabled) {
                            this.expandGridCardName(event);
                        }
                    },

                    requestGridCardHover() {
                        if (this.compact || ! this.hoverIntentEnabled || this.isMobile || this.isHoverActive) {
                            return;
                        }

                        window.clearTimeout(this.hoverIntentTimer);

                        if (performance.now() < productCardHoverBlockedUntil) {
                            return;
                        }

                        this.hoverIntentTimer = window.setTimeout(() => {
                            const card = this.$refs.gridCard;

                            if (
                                ! card
                                || ! card.matches(':hover')
                                || performance.now() < productCardHoverBlockedUntil
                            ) {
                                return;
                            }

                            this.isHoverActive = true;
                            this.$nextTick(() => this.expandGridCardName({ currentTarget: card }));
                        }, productCardHoverIntentDelay);
                    },

                    deactivateGridCardHover(event = null) {
                        window.clearTimeout(this.hoverIntentTimer);
                        this.hoverIntentTimer = null;
                        this.isHoverActive = false;
                        this.collapseGridCardName(event);
                    },

                    observeActionItemsHeight() {
                        const card = this.$refs.gridCard;
                        const actionItems = this.$refs.actionItems;

                        if (! card || ! actionItems) {
                            return;
                        }

                        const updateHeight = () => {
                            const height = Math.ceil(actionItems.getBoundingClientRect().height);

                            card.style.setProperty('--product-card-actions-height', `${height}px`);
                        };

                        updateHeight();

                        if ('ResizeObserver' in window) {
                            this.actionItemsResizeObserver = new ResizeObserver(updateHeight);
                            this.actionItemsResizeObserver.observe(actionItems);
                        }
                    },

                    openProductPage(event, forceNavigation = false) {
                        const selection = window.getSelection();
                        const hasSelectedName = selection
                            && ! selection.isCollapsed
                            && event?.currentTarget?.contains(selection.anchorNode);
                        const wasLongPress = Date.now() - this.namePressStartedAt >= 500;

                        if (! forceNavigation && (wasLongPress || hasSelectedName)) {
                            return;
                        }

                        window.location.assign(`https://yumi.market/${this.product.url_key}`);
                    },

                    expandGridCardName(event) {
                        if (this.compact || this.isMobile) {
                            return;
                        }

                        const card = event.currentTarget;
                        const collapsedHeight = card.getBoundingClientRect().height;

                        card.classList.add('is-name-expanded');

                        const expandedHeight = card.getBoundingClientRect().height;
                        const overflowHeight = Math.max(0, expandedHeight - collapsedHeight);

                        if (card.classList.contains('product-carousel-card')) {
                            card.style.setProperty(
                                '--product-card-name-overflow',
                                `${overflowHeight}px`
                            );

                            return;
                        }

                        card.style.marginBottom = overflowHeight > 0
                            ? `-${overflowHeight}px`
                            : '';
                    },

                    collapseGridCardName(event) {
                        const card = event?.currentTarget || this.$refs.gridCard;

                        if (! card) {
                            return;
                        }

                        card.classList.remove('is-name-expanded');
                        card.style.removeProperty('--product-card-name-overflow');
                        card.style.marginBottom = '';
                    },

                    onMobileSnapScroll(refName) {
                        const el = this.$refs[refName];
                        if (!el) return;

                        const w = el.clientWidth || 1;
                        this.mobileSlideIndex = Math.round(el.scrollLeft / w);
                    },

                    isColor(icon) {
                        return typeof icon === 'string' && icon.startsWith('#');
                    },

                    iconUrl(icon) {
                        if (!icon || this.isColor(icon)) return null;

                        // если уже полный URL
                        if (icon.startsWith('http://') || icon.startsWith('https://')) return icon;

                        // Bagisto swatch_value обычно типа "attribute_option/xxx.png"
                        return `/storage/${icon}`;
                    },

                    formatPrice(value) {
                        if (!value) return '';

                        const formatted = new Intl.NumberFormat('uk', {
                            style: 'currency',
                            currency: 'UAH',
                        }).format(value).replace(',', '.');

                        // Прибираємо пробіл між числом і символом валюти
                        return formatted.replace(/\s+/g, '');
                    },

                    normalizeQty() {
                        // дозволяємо пусте значення під час редагування
                        if (this.selectedQty === '' || this.selectedQty === null || this.selectedQty === undefined) {
                            this.selectedQty = 1; // якщо це blur — повертаємо 1
                            return;
                        }

                        let q = Number(this.selectedQty);
                        if (!Number.isFinite(q)) q = 1;
                        q = Math.trunc(q);
                        if (q < 1) q = 1;
                        if (q > this.maxQty) q = this.maxQty;
                        this.selectedQty = q;
                    },

                    incQty() {
                        if (this.selectedQty < this.maxQty) this.selectedQty++;
                    },

                    decQty() {
                        if (this.selectedQty > 1) this.selectedQty--;
                    },

                    canAddQuickQty(amount) {
                        const currentQty = Math.max(Math.trunc(Number(this.selectedQty)) || 1, 1);
                        const quickQty = Math.trunc(Number(amount));

                        return quickQty > 0 && currentQty + quickQty <= this.maxQty;
                    },

                    addQuickQty(amount) {
        const quickQty = Math.trunc(Number(amount));

        if (! Number.isInteger(quickQty) || quickQty < 1) {
            return;
        }

        const currentQty = Math.max(
            Math.trunc(Number(this.selectedQty)) || 1,
            1
        );

        const nextQty = currentQty === 1
            ? quickQty
            : currentQty + quickQty;

        if (nextQty > this.maxQty) {
            return;
        }

        this.selectedQty = nextQty;
    },

                    onImageClick(event) {
                        if (this.disableImageSwipe) {
                            return;
                        }

                        // якщо перед цим був swipe — блокуємо перехід по посиланню
                        if (this.didSwipeOnImage) {
                            event.preventDefault();
                            event.stopPropagation();

                            // скидаємо прапорець після свайпу
                            this.didSwipeOnImage = false;
                        }
                    },

                    onImageTouchStart() {
                        if (! this.disableImageSwipe) {
                            this.didSwipeOnImage = false;
                        }
                    },

                    onImageTouchMove() {
                        if (! this.disableImageSwipe) {
                            this.didSwipeOnImage = true;
                        }
                    },

                    addToWishlist() {
                        if (this.isCustomer) {
                            this.$axios.post(`https://yumi.market/api/customer/wishlist`, { product_id: this.product.id })
                                .then(response => {
                                    this.product.is_wishlist = ! this.product.is_wishlist;
                                    this.$emitter.emit('update-wishlist', {
                                        delta: this.product.is_wishlist ? 1 : -1,
                                    });
                                    this.$emitter.emit('add-flash', { type: 'success', message: response.data.data.message });
                                })
                                .catch(() => {});
                        } else {
                            window.location.href = "https://yumi.market/customer/login";
                        }
                    },

                    addToCompare(productId) {
                        if (this.isCustomer) {
                            this.$axios.post('https://yumi.market/api/compare-items', { 'product_id': productId })
                                .then(response => this.$emitter.emit('add-flash', { type: 'success', message: response.data.data.message }))
                                .catch(error => {
                                    if ([400, 422].includes(error.response.status)) {
                                        this.$emitter.emit('add-flash', { type: 'warning', message: error.response.data.data.message });
                                        return;
                                    }
                                    this.$emitter.emit('add-flash', { type: 'error', message: error.response.data.message});
                                });
                            return;
                        }

                        let items = this.getStorageValue() ?? [];
                        if (items.length) {
                            if (!items.includes(productId)) {
                                items.push(productId);
                                localStorage.setItem('compare_items', JSON.stringify(items));
                                this.$emitter.emit('add-flash', { type: 'success', message: "Товар успішно додано до списку порівнянь." });
                            } else {
                                this.$emitter.emit('add-flash', { type: 'warning', message: "Товар вже додано до списку порівнянь." });
                            }
                        } else {
                            localStorage.setItem('compare_items', JSON.stringify([productId]));
                            this.$emitter.emit('add-flash', { type: 'success', message: "Товар успішно додано до списку порівнянь." });
                        }
                    },

                    getStorageValue() {
                        let value = localStorage.getItem('compare_items');
                        return value ? JSON.parse(value) : [];
                    },

                    addToCart() {
                        this.normalizeQty();

                        this.isAddingToCart = true;

                        this.$axios.post('https://yumi.market/api/checkout/cart', {
                            'quantity': this.selectedQty,
                            'product_id': this.product.id,
                        })
                            .then(response => {
                                if (response.data.message) {
                                    this.$emitter.emit('update-mini-cart', response.data.data );
                                    this.$emitter.emit('add-flash', { type: 'success', message: response.data.message });
                                } else {
                                    this.$emitter.emit('add-flash', { type: 'warning', message: response.data.data.message });
                                }
                                this.isAddingToCart = false;
                            })
                            .catch(error => {
                                this.$emitter.emit('add-flash', { type: 'error', message: error.response?.data?.message ?? 'Помилка' });
                                if (error.response?.data?.redirect_uri) {
                                    window.location.href = error.response.data.redirect_uri;
                                }
                                this.isAddingToCart = false;
                            });
                    },
                },
            });
        </script>
            <script
        type="text/x-template"
        id="v-product-carousel-template"
    >
        <div class="relative m-auto flex w-full max-w-[600px] overflow-hidden">
            <!-- Slider -->
            <div
                class="inline-flex aspect-square w-full max-h-[600px] translate-x-0 cursor-pointer transition-transform duration-700 ease-out will-change-transform"
                ref="sliderContainer"
            >
                <div
    class="relative grid aspect-square w-full shrink-0 content-center bg-cover bg-no-repeat"
    v-for="(media, index) in options"
    ref="slide"
>
    <!-- Placements overlay (mobile) -->
    <div v-if="index === 0" class="absolute inset-0 z-30 pointer-events-none">
        <!-- TOP LEFT -->
        <div
            v-if="placements?.top_left?.length"
            class="absolute top-0 left-3 flex flex-col gap-2 items-start"
        >
            <template v-for="(item, i) in placements.top_left" :key="`m_tl_${i}`">
                <div class="inline-flex items-center gap-2 rounded-lg px-2 py-1 text-md text-gray-900">
                    <span
                        v-if="item.icon && item.icon.startsWith('#')"
                        class="h-3 w-3 rounded-sm"
                        :style="{ backgroundColor: item.icon }"
                    ></span>

                   <img v-else-if="item.icon && item.is_visible_icons_on_card" class="h-4 w-4" :src="iconUrl(item.icon)" alt="" />

                    <span v-text="item.value"></span>
                </div>
            </template>
        </div>

        <!-- TOP RIGHT -->
        <div
            v-if="placements?.top_right?.length"
            class="absolute top-0 right-3 flex flex-col gap-2 items-end text-right"
        >
            <template v-for="(item, i) in placements.top_right" :key="`m_tr_${i}`">
                <div class="inline-flex items-center gap-2 rounded-lg px-2 py-1 text-md text-gray-900">
                    <span
                        v-if="item.icon && item.icon.startsWith('#')"
                        class="h-3 w-3 rounded-sm"
                        :style="{ backgroundColor: item.icon }"
                    ></span>

                    <img v-else-if="item.icon && item.is_visible_icons_on_card" class="h-4 w-4" :src="iconUrl(item.icon)" alt="" />

                    <span v-text="item.value"></span>
                </div>
            </template>
        </div>

        <!-- BOTTOM LEFT -->
        <div
            v-if="placements?.bottom_left?.length"
            class="absolute bottom-3 left-3 flex flex-col gap-2 items-start"
        >
            <template v-for="(item, i) in placements.bottom_left" :key="`m_bl_${i}`">
                <div class="inline-flex items-center gap-2 rounded-lg px-2 py-1 text-md text-gray-900">
                    <span
                        v-if="item.icon && item.icon.startsWith('#')"
                        class="h-3 w-3 rounded-sm"
                        :style="{ backgroundColor: item.icon }"
                    ></span>

                    <img v-else-if="item.icon && item.is_visible_icons_on_card" class="h-4 w-4" :src="iconUrl(item.icon)" alt="" />

                    <span v-text="item.value"></span>
                </div>
            </template>
        </div>

        <!-- BOTTOM RIGHT -->
        <div
            v-if="placements?.bottom_right?.length"
            class="absolute bottom-3 right-3 flex flex-col gap-2 items-end text-right"
        >
            <template v-for="(item, i) in placements.bottom_right" :key="`m_br_${i}`">
                <div class="inline-flex items-center gap-2 rounded-lg px-2 py-1 text-md text-gray-900">
                    <span
                        v-if="item.icon && item.icon.startsWith('#')"
                        class="h-3 w-3 rounded-sm"
                        :style="{ backgroundColor: item.icon }"
                    ></span>

                    <img v-else-if="item.icon && item.is_visible_icons_on_card" class="h-4 w-4" :src="iconUrl(item.icon)" alt="" />

                    <span v-text="item.value"></span>
                </div>
            </template>
        </div>
    </div>

    <template v-if="media.type == 'videos'">
        <video
            controls
            height="100%"
            :alt="media.video_url"
            :key="media.video_url"
        >
            <source :src="media.video_url" type="video/mp4" />
        </video>
    </template>

    <template v-else>
       <img
    class="h-full w-full max-w-full select-none object-contain transition-transform duration-300 ease-in-out"
    :class="index === 0 ? 'p-8 sm:p-0' : ''"
    :src="media.large_image_url"
    :alt="media.large_image_url"
/>
    </template>
</div>
            </div>

            <!-- Pagination -->
            <div
                class="absolute bottom-3 left-0 flex w-full justify-center max-sm:bottom-2"
                v-if="options?.length > 1"
            >
                <div
                    v-for="(media, index) in options"
                    class="mx-1 h-1.5 w-1.5 cursor-pointer rounded-full"
                    :class="{ 'bg-navyBlue': index === Math.abs(currentIndex), 'opacity-30 bg-gray-500': index !== Math.abs(currentIndex) }"
                    role="button"
                >
                </div>
            </div>
        </div>
    </script>

    <script type="module">
        app.component("v-product-carousel", {
            template: '#v-product-carousel-template',

            props: ['options', 'placements'],

            data() {
                return {
                    isDragging: false,
                    startPos: 0,
                    currentTranslate: 0,
                    prevTranslate: 0,
                    animationID: 0,
                    currentIndex: 0,
                    slider: '',
                    slides: [],
                    autoPlayInterval: null,
                    direction: 'ltr',
                    startFrom: 1,
                    viewportWidth: window.innerWidth,
                };
            },

            mounted() {
                this.slider = this.$refs.sliderContainer;

                if (
                    this.$refs.slide
                    && typeof this.$refs.slide[Symbol.iterator] === 'function'
                ) {
                    this.slides = Array.from(this.$refs.slide);
                }

                this.init();

                window.addEventListener('resize', this.onResize);
            },

            watch: {
                options: function() {
                    this.slider = this.$refs.sliderContainer;

                    if (
                        this.$refs.slide
                        && typeof this.$refs.slide[Symbol.iterator] === 'function'
                    ) {
                        this.slides = Array.from(this.$refs.slide);
                    }

                    this.resetIndex();

                    this.init();
                }
            },

            methods: {
                init() {
                    this.direction = document.dir;

                    if (this.direction === 'rtl') {
                        this.startFrom = -1;
                    }

                    this.slides.forEach((slide, index) => {
                        slide.querySelector('img')?.addEventListener('dragstart', (e) => e.preventDefault());

                        slide.addEventListener('touchstart', this.handleDragStart);

                        slide.addEventListener('touchend', this.handleDragEnd);

                        slide.addEventListener('touchmove', this.handleDrag, { passive: true });
                    });

                    this.setPositionByIndex();
                },

                isColor(icon) {
    return typeof icon === 'string' && icon.startsWith('#');
},

iconUrl(icon) {
    if (!icon || this.isColor(icon)) return null;

    if (icon.startsWith('http://') || icon.startsWith('https://')) return icon;

    return `/storage/${icon}`;
},

                resetIndex() {
                    if (this.currentIndex >= this.slides.length) {

                        this.currentIndex = this.slides.length - 1;
                    }

                    this.setPositionByIndex();
                },

                handleDragStart(event) {
                    this.startPos = event.type === 'mousedown' ? event.clientX : event.touches[0].clientX;

                    this.isDragging = true;

                    this.animationID = requestAnimationFrame(this.animation);
                },

                handleDrag(event) {
                    if (! this.isDragging) {
                        return;
                    }

                    const currentPosition = event.type === 'mousemove' ? event.clientX : event.touches[0].clientX;

                    this.currentTranslate = this.prevTranslate + currentPosition - this.startPos;
                },

                handleDragEnd(event) {
                    clearInterval(this.autoPlayInterval);

                    cancelAnimationFrame(this.animationID);

                    this.isDragging = false;

                    const movedBy = this.currentTranslate - this.prevTranslate;

                    if (this.direction === 'ltr') {
                        if (
                            movedBy < -100
                            && this.currentIndex < this.slides.length - 1
                        ) {
                            this.currentIndex += 1;
                        }

                        if (
                            movedBy > 100
                            && this.currentIndex > 0
                        ) {
                            this.currentIndex -= 1;
                        }
                    } else {
                        if (
                            movedBy > 100
                            && this.currentIndex < this.slides.length - 1
                        ) {
                            if (Math.abs(this.currentIndex) !== this.slides.length - 1) {
                                this.currentIndex -= 1;
                            }
                        }

                        if (
                            movedBy < -100
                            && this.currentIndex < 0
                        ) {
                            this.currentIndex += 1;
                        }
                    }

                    this.setPositionByIndex();
                },

                animation() {
                    this.setSliderPosition();

                    if (this.isDragging) {
                        requestAnimationFrame(this.animation);
                    }
                },

                setPositionByIndex() {
                    this.currentTranslate = this.currentIndex * -this.slideWidth();

                    this.prevTranslate = this.currentTranslate;

                    this.setSliderPosition();
                },

                setSliderPosition() {
                    if (this.slider) {
                        this.slider.style.transform = `translateX(${this.currentTranslate}px)`;
                    }
                },

                onResize() {
                    this.viewportWidth = this.slideWidth();
                    this.setPositionByIndex();
                },

                slideWidth() {
                    return this.slider?.clientWidth || window.innerWidth;
                },
            },
        });
    </script>
    <script
        type="text/x-template"
        id="v-gallery-zoomer-template"
    >
        <transition
            tag="div"
            class="bg-white"
            name="modal-content"
            enter-class="duration-300 ease-out"
            enter-from-class="translate-y-4 opacity-0 md:translate-y-0 md:scale-95"
            enter-to-class="translate-y-0 opacity-100 md:scale-100"
            leave-class="duration-200 ease-in"
            leave-from-class="translate-y-0 opacity-100 md:scale-100"
            leave-to-class="translate-y-4 opacity-0 md:translate-y-0 md:scale-95"
        >
            <div
                ref="parentContainer"
                class="fixed inset-0 z-20 flex transform flex-col gap-4 overflow-y-auto transition"
                v-show="isOpen"
            >
                <!-- Close -->
                <span
                    class="icon-cancel absolute top-3 z-[1000] cursor-pointer text-3xl ltr:right-3 rtl:left-3"
                    @click="toggle"
                >
                </span>

                <span
                    class="icon-arrow-left fixed left-2.5 top-1/2 z-10 -mt-12 w-auto cursor-pointer rounded-full bg-[rgba(0,0,0,0.8)] p-3 text-2xl font-bold text-white opacity-30 transition-all hover:opacity-100"
                    v-if="attachments.length >= 2"
                    @click="navigate(currentIndex -= 1)"
                >
                </span>

                <span
                    class="icon-arrow-right fixed right-2.5 top-1/2 z-10 -mt-12 w-auto cursor-pointer rounded-full bg-[rgba(0,0,0,0.8)] p-3 text-2xl font-bold text-white opacity-30 transition-all hover:opacity-100"
                    v-if="attachments.length >= 2"
                    @click="navigate(currentIndex += 1)"
                >
                </span>

                <!-- Main Image -->
                <div
                    ref="mediaContainer"
                    class="h-full w-full overflow-hidden"
                >
                    <div
                        class="relative m-auto flex w-full items-center justify-center"
                        :class="{
                            'h-full': ! isZooming,
                            'h-auto': isZooming
                        }"
                    >
                        <div
                            v-for="(attachment, index) in attachments"
                            class="h-full items-center justify-center"
                            ref="slides"
                        >
                            <video
                                class="max-h-full max-w-full transition-transform duration-300 ease-out"
                                controls
                                v-if="attachment.type == 'video'"
                            >
                                <source :src="attachment.url" type="video/mp4">
                                <source :src="attachment.url" type="video/ogg">
                                    Your browser does not support HTML video.
                            </video>

                            <template v-if="attachment.type === 'image'">
                                <!-- For Desktop -->
                                <img
                                    :src="attachment.url"
                                    class="max-h-full max-w-full transition-transform duration-300 ease-out max-md:hidden"
                                    :class="{
                                        'cursor-zoom-in': ! isZooming,
                                        'cursor-grab': ! isDragging && isZooming,
                                        'cursor-grabbing': isDragging && isZooming,
                                    }"
                                    :style="{transform: `translate(${translateX}px, ${translateY}px)`}"
                                    @click.stop="handleClick"
                                    @mousedown.prevent="handleMouseDown"
                                    @mousemove.prevent="handleMouseMove"
                                    @mouseleave.prevent="resetImagePosition"
                                    @mouseup.prevent="resetImagePosition"
                                    @mousewheel="handleMouseWheel"
                                />

                                <!-- For Mobile -->
                                <img
                                    :src="attachment.url"
                                    class="max-h-full max-w-full transition-transform duration-300 ease-out md:hidden"
                                    :class="{
                                        'cursor-zoom-in': ! isZooming,
                                        'cursor-grab': ! isDragging && isZooming,
                                        'cursor-grabbing': isDragging && isZooming,
                                    }"
                                    :style="{transform: `translate(${translateX}px, ${translateY}px)`}"
                                />
                            </template>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                </div>

                <!-- Thumbnails -->
                <div class="mb-4 flex justify-center gap-x-2">
                    <template v-for="(attachment, index) in attachments">
                        <img
                            class="h-16 w-16 transform cursor-pointer rounded-md border border-navyBlue border-transparent object-cover transition-transform hover:!border-navyBlue"
                            :class="{
                                '!border-navyBlue': currentIndex === index + 1,
                            }"
                            :src="attachment.url"
                            :key="index"
                            v-if="attachment.type === 'image'"
                            @click="navigate(currentIndex = index + 1)"
                        />

                        <video
                            class="h-16 w-16 transform cursor-pointer rounded-md border border-navyBlue border-transparent object-cover transition-transform hover:!border-navyBlue"
                            :class="{
                                '!border-navyBlue': currentIndex === index + 1,
                            }"
                            :src="attachment.url"
                            :key="index"
                            v-if="attachment.type === 'video'"
                            @click="navigate(currentIndex = index + 1)"
                        />
                    </template>
                </div>
            </div>
        </transition>
    </script>

    <script type="module">
        app.component('v-gallery-zoomer', {
            template: '#v-gallery-zoomer-template',

            props: {
                attachments: {
                    type: Object,

                    required: true,

                    default: () => [],
                },

                isImageZooming: {
                    type: Boolean,

                    default: false,
                },

                initialIndex: {
                    type: String,

                    default: 0,
                },
            },

            watch: {
                isImageZooming(newVal, oldVal) {
                    this.currentIndex = parseInt(this.initialIndex.split('_').pop()) + 1;

                    this.navigate(this.currentIndex);

                    this.toggle();
                },
            },

            data() {
                return {
                    isOpen: this.isImageZooming,

                    isDragging: false,

                    isZooming: false,

                    currentIndex: 1,

                    startDragX: 0,

                    startDragY: 0,

                    translateX: 0,

                    translateY: 0,

                    isMouseMoveTriggered: false,

                    isMouseDownTriggered: false,
                };
            },

            methods: {
                toggle() {
                    this.isOpen = ! this.isOpen;

                    document.body.style.overflow = this.isOpen ? 'hidden' : '';
                },

                open() {
                    this.isOpen = true;

                    document.body.style.overflow = 'hidden';
                },

                navigate(index) {
                    if (index > this.attachments.length) {
                        this.currentIndex = 1;
                    }

                    if (index < 1) {
                        this.currentIndex = this.attachments.length;
                    }

                    let slides = this.$refs.slides;

                    for (let i = 0; i < slides.length; i++) {
                        if (i == this.currentIndex - 1) {
                            continue;
                        }

                        slides[i].style.display = 'none';
                    }

                    slides[this.currentIndex - 1].style.display = 'flex';

                    this.isZooming = false;

                    this.resetDrag();
                },

                handleClick(event) {
                    if (
                        this.isMouseMoveTriggered
                        && ! this.isMouseDownTriggered
                    ) {
                        return;
                    }

                    this.resetDrag();

                    this.isZooming = ! this.isZooming;
                },

                handleOuterClick() {
                    if (! this.isZooming) {
                        return;
                    }

                    this.isZooming = false;

                    resetDrag();
                },

                handleMouseDown(event) {
                    this.isMouseDownTriggered = true;

                    this.isDragging = true;

                    this.startDragX = event.clientX;

                    this.startDragY = event.clientY;
                },

                handleMouseMove(event) {
                    this.isMouseMoveTriggered = true;

                    this.isMouseDownTriggered = false;

                    if (! this.isDragging) {
                        return;
                    }

                    const deltaX = event.clientX - this.startDragX;

                    const deltaY = event.clientY - this.startDragY;

                    const newTranslateY = this.translateY + deltaY;

                    const remainingHeight = this.$refs.parentContainer.clientHeight - this.$refs.mediaContainer.clientHeight;

                    const maxTranslateY = Math.min(0, window.innerHeight - (event.srcElement.height + remainingHeight));

                    const clampedTranslateY = Math.max(maxTranslateY, Math.min(newTranslateY, 0));

                    this.translateY = clampedTranslateY;

                    this.startDragY = event.clientY;

                    this.startDragX = event.clientX;

                    this.translateX += deltaX;
                },

                handleMouseWheel(event) {
                    const deltaY = event.clientY - this.startDragY;

                    let newTranslateY = this.translateY - event.deltaY / Math.abs(event.deltaY) * 100;

                    const remainingHeight = this.$refs.parentContainer.clientHeight - this.$refs.mediaContainer.clientHeight;

                    const maxTranslateY = Math.min(0, window.innerHeight - (event.srcElement.height + remainingHeight));

                    this.translateY = Math.max(maxTranslateY, Math.min(newTranslateY, 0));
                },

                resetImagePosition() {
                    this.isDragging = false;

                    this.translateX  = 0;

                    this.startDragX = 0;
                },

                resetDrag() {
                    this.isDragging = false;

                    this.startDragX = 0;

                    this.startDragY = 0;

                    this.translateX = 0;

                    this.translateY = 0;
                },
            },
        });
    </script>
        <script
            type="text/x-template"
            id="v-product-customizable-options-template"
        >
            <div class="max-sm:mt-0 max-w-[470px]">
                <template v-for="(option, index) in options">
                    <v-product-customizable-option-item
                        :option="option"
                        :key="index"
                        @priceUpdated="priceUpdated"
                    >
                    </v-product-customizable-option-item>
                </template>
                <div class="mb-2.5 flex items-center justify-between">
                        <p class="text-sm">Від 50 шт</p>
                        <p class="text-xl font-medium max-sm:text-xl text-red-500">4.74₴</p>
                    </div>

                <div class="mb-2.5 mt-5 hidden flex items-center justify-between" v-if="!madeToOrder">

                        На складі                    </p>

                            :class="[
                              'inline-block rounded-lg px-2.5 py-1 bg-opacity-70 text-sm max-sm:rounded-l-none max-sm:rounded-r-xl max-sm:px-2 max-sm:py-0.5 max-sm:text-xs ltr:left-1.5 max-sm:ltr:left-0 rtl:right-1.5 max-sm:rtl:right-0 text-stone-800',
                              flatQty > 0 ? 'bg-gray-100' : 'hidden'
                            ]"
                        >
                            {{ flatQty }} шт                        </span>

                </div>

        </script>

        <script type="module">
            app.component('v-product-customizable-options', {
                template: '#v-product-customizable-options-template',

                props: {
                    madeToOrder: {
                        type: Boolean,
                        default: false,
                    },
                    initialPrice: {
                        type: Number,
                        required: true,
                    },
                    flatQty: {
                        type: Number,
                        required: true,
                    },
                },

                data: function() {
                    return {
                        options: [],

                        prices: [],
                    }
                },

                mounted() {
                    this.options = this.options.map((option) => {
                        if (! this.canHaveMultiplePriceOptions(option.type)) {
                            return {
                                id: option.id,
                                label: option.label,
                                type: option.type,
                                is_required: option.is_required,
                                max_characters: option.max_characters,
                                supported_file_extensions: option.supported_file_extensions,
                                price_id: option.customizable_option_prices[0].id,
                                price: option.customizable_option_prices[0].price,
                            };
                        }

                        return {
                            id: option.id,
                            label: option.label,
                            type: option.type,
                            is_required: option.is_required,
                            max_characters: option.max_characters,
                            supported_file_extensions: option.supported_file_extensions,
                            price: 0,
                        };
                    });

                    this.prices = this.options.map((option) => {
                        return {
                            option_id: option.id,
                            price: 0,
                        };
                    });
                },

                computed: {
                    formattedTotalPrice: function() {
                        let totalPrice = this.initialPrice;

                        for (let price of this.prices) {
                            totalPrice += parseFloat(price.price);
                        }

                        return this.$shop.formatPrice(totalPrice);
                    }
                },

                methods: {
                    priceUpdated({ option, totalPrice }) {
                        let price = this.prices.find(price => price.option_id === option.id);

                        price.price = totalPrice;
                    },

                    canHaveMultiplePriceOptions(type) {
                        return ['checkbox', 'radio', 'select', 'multiselect'].includes(type);
                    },
                }
            });

            app.component('v-product-customizable-option-item', {
                template: '#v-product-customizable-option-item-template',

                emits: ['priceUpdated'],

                props: ['option'],

                data: function() {
                    return {
                        optionItems: [],

                        selectedItems: this.canHaveMultiplePrices()  ? [] : null,
                    };
                },

                mounted() {
                    if (! this.option.customizable_option_prices) {
                        return;
                    }

                    this.optionItems = this.option.customizable_option_prices.map(optionItem => {
                        return {
                            id: optionItem.id,
                            label: optionItem.label,
                            price: optionItem.price,
                        };
                    });
                },

                watch: {
                    selectedItems: function (value) {
                        let selectedItemValues = Array.isArray(value) ? value : [value];

                        let totalPrice = 0;

                        for (let item of this.optionItems) {
                            switch (this.option.type) {
                                case 'text':
                                case 'textarea':
                                case 'date':
                                case 'datetime':
                                case 'time':
                                    if (selectedItemValues[0].length > 0) {
                                        totalPrice += parseFloat(item.price);
                                    }

                                    break;

                                case 'checkbox':
                                case 'radio':
                                case 'select':
                                case 'multiselect':
                                    if (selectedItemValues.includes(item.id)) {
                                        totalPrice += parseFloat(item.price);
                                    }

                                case 'file':
                                    if (selectedItemValues[0] instanceof File) {
                                        totalPrice += parseFloat(item.price);
                                    }

                                    break;
                            }
                        }

                        this.$emit('priceUpdated', {
                            option: this.option,

                            totalPrice,
                        });
                    },
                },

                methods: {
                    canHaveMultiplePrices() {
                        return ['checkbox', 'multiselect'].includes(this.option.type);
                    },

                    handleFileChange($event) {
                        const selectedFiles = event.target.files;

                        this.selectedItems = selectedFiles[0];
                    },
                },
            });
        </script>
        <script
        type="text/x-template"
        id="v-facebook-share-template"
    >
        <li
            class="group relative flex size-14 shrink-0 cursor-pointer items-center justify-center
                   rounded-lg border p-0 text-sm text-gray-600 transition-all
                   hover:border-gray-400 hover:text-gray-800
                   focus-within:border-gray-400
                   dark:border-gray-800 dark:bg-gray-900 dark:text-gray-300
                   dark:hover:border-gray-400 dark:focus-within:border-gray-400
                   max-sm:size-12 max-sm:text-base"
        >
            <a
                class="flex h-full w-full items-center justify-center"
                :href="shareUrl"
                @click.prevent="openSharePopup"
                aria-label="Facebook"
                role="button"
                tabindex="0"
            >
                <span class="icon-facebook text-3xl"></span>
            </a>

            <span
                class="pointer-events-none absolute bottom-full left-1/2 z-50 mb-2
                       -translate-x-1/2 whitespace-nowrap rounded-md bg-black
                       px-2.5 py-1.5 text-xs text-white opacity-0 shadow-md
                       transition-opacity duration-200
                       group-hover:opacity-100
                       group-focus-within:opacity-100"
            >
                Facebook
            </span>
        </li>
    </script>

    <script type="module">
        app.component('v-facebook-share', {
            template: '#v-facebook-share-template',

            data() {
                return {
                    shareUrl: "https:\/\/www.facebook.com\/sharer\/sharer.php?u=https%3A%2F%2Fyumi.market%2Fkalisan-kl-11-14-satin-snizno-bilii-opaque-satin-snow-white%3Flocale%3Duk%26currency%3DUAH",
                };
            },

            methods: {
                openSharePopup() {
                    window.open(
                        this.shareUrl,
                        'facebook-share',
                        'resizable=yes,scrollbars=yes,width=500,height=500'
                    );
                },

            class='fixed bottom-10 left-1/2 z-[1001] grid -translate-x-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 transform justify-items-center gap-2.5 sm:hidden'
        >

    <script type="module">
        app.component('v-flash-group', {
            template: '#v-flash-group-template',

            data() {
                return {
                    uid: 0,

                    flashes: []
                }
            },

            created() {

                                                                                                                                                                
                this.registerGlobalEvents();
            },

            methods: {
               add(flash) {
                    if (
                        window.innerWidth < 640
                        && flash.type === 'success'
                        && flash.message === 'Товар успішно додано'
                    ) {
                        return;
                    }

                    flash.uid = this.uid++;

                    this.flashes.push(flash);
                },

                remove(flash) {
                    let index = this.flashes.indexOf(flash);

                    this.flashes.splice(index, 1);
                },

                registerGlobalEvents() {
                    this.$emitter.on('add-flash', this.add);
                },
            }
        });
    </script>
