    <script type="module">
        app.component('v-modal-confirm', {
            template: '#v-modal-confirm-template',

            data() {
                return {
                    isOpen: false,

                    title: '',

                    message: '',

                    options: {
                        btnDisagree: '',
                        btnAgree: '',
                    },

                    agreeCallback: null,

                    disagreeCallback: null,
                };
            },

            created() {
                this.registerGlobalEvents();
            },

            methods: {
                open({
                    title = "Ви впевнені?",
                    message = "Ви впевнені, що хочете виконати цю дію?",
                    options = {
                        btnDisagree: "Відмовитися",
                        btnAgree: "Погодитися",
                    },
                    agree = () => {},
                    disagree = () => {},
                }) {
                    this.isOpen = true;

                    const scrollbarWidth = window.innerWidth - document.documentElement.clientWidth;

                    document.body.style.overflow = 'hidden';

                    document.body.style.paddingRight = `${scrollbarWidth}px`;

                    this.title = title;

                    this.message = message;

                    this.options = options;

                    this.agreeCallback = agree;

                    this.disagreeCallback = disagree;
                },

                disagree() {
                    this.isOpen = false;

                    document.body.style.overflow = 'auto';

                    document.body.style.paddingRight = '';

                    this.disagreeCallback();
                },

                agree() {
                    this.isOpen = false;

                    document.body.style.overflow = 'auto';

                    document.body.style.paddingRight = '';

                    this.agreeCallback();
                },

                registerGlobalEvents() {
                    this.$emitter.on('open-confirm-modal', this.open);
                },
            }
        });
    </script>
    <script type="module">
        window.yumiCategoryTreeLoader = window.yumiCategoryTreeLoader || {
            data: null,
            promise: null,

            load(axios) {
                if (this.data !== null) {
                    return Promise.resolve(this.data);
                }

                if (this.promise) {
                    return this.promise;
                }

                this.promise = axios.get("https:\/\/yumi.market\/api\/yumi\/categories\/tree?locale=uk")
                    .then(response => {
                        this.data = Array.isArray(response.data?.data)
                            ? response.data.data
                            : [];

                        return this.data;
                    })
                    .finally(() => {
                        this.promise = null;
                    });

                return this.promise;
            },
        };
    </script>

        type="text/x-template"

    >
        

                            <div

    <script type="module">
        const secureFlag = "";
        const sameSiteFlag = ";samesite=lax";

        app.component('v-cookie', {
            template: '#v-cookie-template',

            data() {
                return {
                    cookieDomain: 'yumi.market',
                    cookieIp: "104.22.18.5",
                    position: "bottom-left"
                };
            },

            mounted() {
                if (! this.cookieExists()) {
                    this.showCookieDialog();
                }
            },

            methods: {
                getPositionClasses(position) {
                    const positionClasses = {
                        'center': 'left-1/2 top-1/2 -translate-x-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 max-w-[420px]',
                        'top-center': 'top-4 left-1/2 -translate-x-1/2 max-w-[420px]',
                        'bottom-center': 'bottom-4 left-1/2 -translate-x-1/2 max-w-[420px]',
                        'bottom-right': 'bottom-4 right-0 sm:max-w-[420px]',
                        'bottom-left': 'bottom-4 left-0 sm:max-w-[420px]',
                        'top-right': 'top-4 right-0 sm:max-w-[420px]',
                        'top-left': 'top-4 left-0 sm:max-w-[420px]',
                    };

                    return positionClasses[position] || positionClasses['center'];
                },

                createCookie() {
                    this.consentWithCookies();

                    this.acceptAllConsentWithCookies();

                    this.hideCookieDialog();
                },

                rejectCookie() {
                    this.hideCookieDialog();
                },

                setCookie(name, value, expirationInDays) {
                    const date = new Date();

                    date.setTime(date.getTime() + (expirationInDays * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000));

                    document.cookie = `${name}=${value};expires=${date.toUTCString()};path=/${secureFlag}${sameSiteFlag}`;
                },

                consentWithCookies() {
                    this.setCookie('cookie-consent', 1, 365 * 20);
                },

                acceptAllConsentWithCookies() {
                    this.setCookie('ip_address', this.cookieIp, 365 * 20);
                },

                cookieExists() {
                    return document.cookie.includes(`cookie-consent=${1}`);
                },

                hideCookieDialog() {
    const cookieConsentElement = document.querySelector('.js-cookie-consent');

    if (cookieConsentElement) {
        cookieConsentElement.classList.remove('is-visible');
    }
},

showCookieDialog() {
    const cookieConsentElement = document.querySelector('.js-cookie-consent');

    if (cookieConsentElement) {
        window.setTimeout(() => {
            cookieConsentElement.classList.add('is-visible');
        }, 3000);
    }
},


            },
        });
    </script>

                        aria-controls="mini-cart-delivery-details"
                        aria-label="Кошик"
                        @click="toggleMiniCartSummary"
                    >
                        <span
                            class="icon-arrow-up relative z-10 -translate-y-0.5 text-xl transition-transform duration-300 motion-reduce:transition-none"
                            :class="miniCartSummaryExpanded ? 'rotate-180' : 'rotate-0'"
                            aria-hidden="true"
                        ></span>
                    </button>

                    <div
                        id="mini-cart-delivery-details"
                        class="grid transition-all duration-300 ease-out motion-reduce:transition-none"
                        :class="miniCartSummaryExpanded
                            ? 'grid-rows-[1fr] opacity-100'
                            : 'pointer-events-none grid-rows-[0fr] opacity-0'"
                    >
                        <div class="grid min-h-0 gap-2.5 overflow-hidden py-1">
                            <section
                                v-if="cart.delivery_cost?.show_cart_progress"
                                class="border-b border-zinc-200 pb-5"
                                :aria-busy="isLoading ? 'true' : 'false'"
                                :aria-label="cartProgressMessage"
                            >
                                <div class="flex items-center gap-3">
                                    <img
                                        v-if="cart.delivery_cost.progress_type === 'gift'"
                                        src="https://yumi.market/themes/shop/yumi-new/build/assets/gift-BRYXZyrl.svg"
                                        alt=""
                                        width="40"
                                        height="40"
                                        class="h-8 w-8 shrink-0"
                                        aria-hidden="true"
                                    >

                                    <img
                                        v-else
                                        src="https://yumi.market/themes/shop/yumi-new/build/assets/np-symbol-70JflOQK.svg"
                                        alt="Нова пошта"
                                        width="32"
                                        height="32"
                                        class="h-8 w-8 shrink-0"
                                    >

                                    <div
                                        class="min-w-0"
                                        aria-live="polite"
                                        aria-atomic="true"
                                    >
                                        <p class="text-sm font-semibold leading-5">
                                            {{ cartProgressMessage }}
                                        </p>

                                        <p
                                            v-if="cart.delivery_cost.progress_type === 'delivery'"
                                            class="mt-0.5 text-xs leading-4 text-zinc-500"
                                        >
                                            Тільки на відділення або поштомат                                        </p>
                                    </div>
                                </div>

                                <div
                                    class="mt-3 h-2 overflow-hidden rounded-full bg-[#eceef1]"
                                    role="progressbar"
                                    :aria-label="cartProgressMessage"
                                    aria-valuemin="0"
                                    :aria-valuemax="cart.delivery_cost.progress_threshold"
                                    :aria-valuenow="Math.min(cart.delivery_cost.progress_amount, cart.delivery_cost.progress_threshold)"
                                    :aria-valuetext="cartProgressMessage"
                                >
                                    <span
                                        class="block h-full rounded-[inherit] bg-[#55a65a] transition-[width] duration-200 motion-reduce:transition-none"
                                        :style="{ width: cart.delivery_cost.cart_progress_percent + '%' }"
                                    ></span>
                                </div>

                                <p
                                    v-if="! cart.delivery_cost.progress_qualified"
                                    class="mt-2 text-xs font-medium leading-4 text-zinc-500"
                                >
                                    {{ formatDeliveryAmount(cart.delivery_cost.progress_amount) }} / {{ formatDeliveryAmount(cart.delivery_cost.progress_threshold) }}
                                </p>
                            </section>
                        </div>
                    </div>

                    <div class="flex justify-between text-right">
                        

                        <p class="text-lg font-semibold max-md:text-base">
                            Підсумок                        </p>

                        <p class="text-lg font-semibold max-md:text-base">
                            {{ cart.formatted_grand_total }}
                        </p>

                        
                    </div>

                        

                        <!-- Cart Action Container -->
                        <div class="grid gap-0 max-md:mb-4">
                            

                        <a
                            href="https://yumi.market/checkout/onepage"
                            class="primary-button mt-4 w-full max-w-none place-self-end rounded-2xl px-11 py-3 max-md:mt-2.5 max-md:max-w-full max-md:rounded-lg max-md:py-3 max-md:text-sm max-sm:w-full max-sm:py-2"
                        >
                            Перейти до оформлення                        </a>

                            

                            <div
                                class="grid transition-all duration-300 ease-out motion-reduce:transition-none"
                                :class="miniCartSummaryExpanded
                                    ? 'mt-2.5 grid-rows-[1fr] opacity-100 max-sm:mt-1.5'
                                    : 'pointer-events-none mt-0 grid-rows-[0fr] opacity-0'"
                            >
                                <div class="min-h-0 overflow-hidden">
                                    <a
                                        class="secondary-button w-full max-w-none rounded-2xl px-11 py-3 max-md:max-w-full max-md:rounded-lg max-md:py-3 max-md:text-sm max-sm:w-full max-sm:py-2"
                                        href="https://yumi.market/checkout/cart"
                                        :tabindex="miniCartSummaryExpanded ? null : -1"
                                    >
                                        Переглянути кошик                                    </a>
                                </div>
                            </div>
                        </div>

                        
                    </div>
            </div>
        </template>
    </v-drawer>


        
        
    </script>

    <script type="module">
        app.component("v-mini-cart", {
            template: '#v-mini-cart-template',

            data() {
                return  {
                    cart: null,

                    isLoading:false,
                    pendingCartItems: {},
                    cartUpdateTimers: {},
                    cartUpdateVersions: {},
                    cartUpdateSequence: 0,

                    miniCartSummaryExpanded: true,

                    miniCartSummaryTouchStart: null,

                    miniCartSummarySwipeHandled: false,

                    miniCartSummarySwipeTimer: null,

                    displayTax: {
                        prices: "excluding_tax",
                        subtotal: "excluding_tax",
                    },
                }
            },

            mounted() {

                this.getCart();

                /**
                 * To Do: Implement this.
                 *
                 * Action.
                 */
                this.$emitter.on('update-mini-cart', this.updateCart);

                this.$emitter.on('open-mini-cart', this.openMiniCart);

                this.$emitter.on('close-mini-cart', this.closeMiniCart);


            },

            unmounted() {
                Object.values(this.cartUpdateTimers).forEach(timer => window.clearTimeout(timer));

                window.clearTimeout(this.miniCartSummarySwipeTimer);

                this.$emitter.off('update-mini-cart', this.updateCart);
                this.$emitter.off('open-mini-cart', this.openMiniCart);
                this.$emitter.off('close-mini-cart', this.closeMiniCart);
            },

            computed: {
                cartProgressMessage() {
                    if (this.cart?.delivery_cost?.progress_type === 'gift') {
                        if (this.cart.delivery_cost.progress_qualified) {
                            return "\u041f\u043e\u0434\u0430\u0440\u0443\u043d\u043e\u043a \u0434\u043e\u0434\u0430\u043d\u043e \u0434\u043e \u0412\u0430\u0448\u043e\u0433\u043e \u043a\u043e\u0448\u0438\u043a\u0430";
                        }

                        return "\u0429\u0435 :amount \u2014 \u0456 \u043f\u043e\u0434\u0430\u0440\u0443\u043d\u043e\u043a \u0412\u0430\u0448!"                            .replace(':amount', this.formatDeliveryAmount(this.cart.delivery_cost.progress_remaining_amount));
                    }

                    if (this.cart?.delivery_cost?.progress_qualified) {
                        return "\u0411\u0435\u0437\u043a\u043e\u0448\u0442\u043e\u0432\u043d\u0430 \u0434\u043e\u0441\u0442\u0430\u0432\u043a\u0430 \u0434\u043e\u0441\u0442\u0443\u043f\u043d\u0430!";
                    }

                    return "\u0429\u0435 :amount \u2014 \u0456 \u0434\u043e\u0441\u0442\u0430\u0432\u043a\u0430 \u0431\u0435\u0437\u043a\u043e\u0448\u0442\u043e\u0432\u043d\u0430!"                        .replace(':amount', this.formatDeliveryAmount(this.cart?.delivery_cost?.progress_remaining_amount));
                },
            },

            methods: {

                toggleMiniCartSummary() {
                    if (this.miniCartSummarySwipeHandled) {
                        return;
                    }

                    this.miniCartSummaryExpanded = ! this.miniCartSummaryExpanded;
                },

                handleMiniCartSummaryTouchStart(event) {
                    const touch = event.changedTouches?.[0];

                    this.miniCartSummaryTouchStart = touch
                        ? { x: touch.clientX, y: touch.clientY }
                        : null;
                },

                handleMiniCartSummaryTouchEnd(event) {
                    const touch = event.changedTouches?.[0];
                    const touchStart = this.miniCartSummaryTouchStart;

                    this.miniCartSummaryTouchStart = null;

                    if (! touch || ! touchStart || window.innerWidth >= 768) {
                        return;
                    }

                    const deltaX = touch.clientX - touchStart.x;
                    const deltaY = touch.clientY - touchStart.y;

                    if (
                        Math.abs(deltaY) < 40
                        || Math.abs(deltaY) <= Math.abs(deltaX) * 1.25
                    ) {
                        return;
                    }

                    this.miniCartSummaryExpanded = deltaY < 0;
                    this.miniCartSummarySwipeHandled = true;

                    window.clearTimeout(this.miniCartSummarySwipeTimer);

                    this.miniCartSummarySwipeTimer = window.setTimeout(() => {
                        this.miniCartSummarySwipeHandled = false;
                    }, 400);
                },

                formatDeliveryAmount(amount) {
                    return new Intl.NumberFormat("uk", {
                        style: 'currency', currency: 'UAH', minimumFractionDigits: 0, maximumFractionDigits: 2,
                    }).format(Number(amount) || 0);
                },

                updateCart(cart) {
                    this.cart = this.normalizeCart(cart);
                },

                openMiniCart() {
                    this.$refs.miniCartDrawer?.open();
                },

                closeMiniCart() {
                    this.$refs.miniCartDrawer?.close();
                },

                getCart() {

                    window.yumiMiniCartPromise ??= this.$axios
                        .get('https://yumi.market/api/checkout/cart');

                    const request = window.yumiMiniCartPromise;

                    return request
                        .then(response => {

                            const cart = this.normalizeCart(response.data.data);

                            this.cart = cart;

                            this.$emitter.emit('update-mini-cart', cart);


                                                    })
                        .catch(error => {})
                        .finally(() => {
                            if (window.yumiMiniCartPromise === request) {
                                window.yumiMiniCartPromise = null;
                            }
                        });
                },

                updateItem(quantity, item) {
                    const itemId = item?.id;
                    const normalizedQuantity = this.normalizeCartQuantity(quantity);

                    if (! itemId || normalizedQuantity === null) {
                        return;
                    }

                    const version = ++this.cartUpdateSequence;

                    this.cartUpdateVersions[itemId] = version;
                    this.setPendingCartItem(itemId, true);
                    this.setLocalCartItemQuantity(itemId, normalizedQuantity);

                    if (this.cartUpdateTimers[itemId]) {
                        window.clearTimeout(this.cartUpdateTimers[itemId]);
                    }

                    this.cartUpdateTimers[itemId] = window.setTimeout(() => {
                        this.flushCartItemQuantity(itemId, normalizedQuantity, version);
                    }, 350);
                },

                flushCartItemQuantity(itemId, quantity, version) {
                    let qty = {};

                    qty[itemId] = quantity;

                    this.isLoading = true;

                    this.$axios.put('https://yumi.market/api/checkout/cart', { qty })
                        .then(response => {
                            if (this.cartUpdateVersions[itemId] !== version) {
                                return;
                            }

                            if (response.data.message) {
                                this.cart = this.normalizeCart(response.data.data);
                                this.$emitter.emit('update-mini-cart', this.cart);
                            } else {
                                this.$emitter.emit('add-flash', { type: 'warning', message: response.data.data.message });
                            }
                        })
                        .catch(error => {
                            if (this.cartUpdateVersions[itemId] !== version) {
                                return;
                            }

                            this.$emitter.emit('add-flash', {
                                type: 'error',
                                message: error.response?.data?.message || 'Не вдалося оновити кількість товару.',
                            });

                            this.getCart();
                        })
                        .finally(() => {
                            if (this.cartUpdateVersions[itemId] !== version) {
                                return;
                            }

                            delete this.cartUpdateTimers[itemId];
                            this.setPendingCartItem(itemId, false);
                            this.isLoading = Object.values(this.pendingCartItems).some(Boolean);
                        });
                },

                moveItemToWishlist(item) {
                    if (! false) {
                        window.location.href = "https:\/\/yumi.market\/customer\/login";

                        return;
                    }

                    const itemId = item?.id;

                    if (! itemId) {
                        return;
                    }

                    if (this.cartUpdateTimers[itemId]) {
                        window.clearTimeout(this.cartUpdateTimers[itemId]);
                        delete this.cartUpdateTimers[itemId];
                    }

                    delete this.cartUpdateVersions[itemId];
                    this.setPendingCartItem(itemId, true);

                    this.$axios.post('https://yumi.market/api/checkout/cart/move-to-wishlist', {
                        ids: [itemId],
                        qty: [this.getItemQuantity(item)],
                    }).then(response => {
                        this.cart = this.normalizeCart(response.data.data);
                        this.$emitter.emit('update-mini-cart', this.cart);
                        this.$emitter.emit('add-flash', {
                            type: 'success',
                            message: response.data.message,
                        });
                    }).catch(error => {
                        this.$emitter.emit('add-flash', {
                            type: 'error',
                            message: error.response?.data?.message,
                        });
                    }).finally(() => {
                        this.setPendingCartItem(itemId, false);
                    });
                },

                removeItem(itemId) {
                    this.isLoading = true;

                    if (this.cartUpdateTimers[itemId]) {
                        window.clearTimeout(this.cartUpdateTimers[itemId]);
                        delete this.cartUpdateTimers[itemId];
                    }

                    delete this.cartUpdateVersions[itemId];
                    this.pendingCartItems = {
                        ...this.pendingCartItems,
                        [itemId]: false,
                    };

                    this.$axios.post('https://yumi.market/api/checkout/cart', {
                        _method: 'DELETE',
                        cart_item_id: itemId,
                    })
                    .then(response => {
                        this.cart = this.normalizeCart(response.data.data);
                        this.$emitter.emit('update-mini-cart', this.cart);


                        this.$emitter.emit('add-flash', {
                            type: 'success',
                            message: response.data.message,
                        });

                        this.isLoading = false;
                    })
                    .catch(error => {
                        this.$emitter.emit('add-flash', {
                            type: 'error',
                            message: error.response?.data?.message || 'Не вдалося видалити товар з кошика.',
                        });

                        this.isLoading = false;
                    });
                },

                normalizeCartQuantity(quantity) {
                    const value = Number(quantity);

                    if (! Number.isFinite(value)) {
                        return null;
                    }

                    return Math.max(1, Math.trunc(value));
                },

                setPendingCartItem(itemId, isPending) {
                    this.pendingCartItems = {
                        ...this.pendingCartItems,
                        [itemId]: isPending,
                    };

                    this.isLoading = Object.values(this.pendingCartItems).some(Boolean);
                },

                setLocalCartItemQuantity(itemId, quantity) {
                    if (! this.cart?.items) {
                        return;
                    }

                    this.cart.items = this.cart.items.map(item => {
                        if (item.id !== itemId) {
                            return item;
                        }

                        return {
                            ...item,
                            quantity,
                        };
                    });

                    this.updateLocalCartItemsQty();
                },

                updateLocalCartItemsQty() {
                    if (! this.cart?.items) {
                        return;
                    }

                    this.cart.items_qty = this.cart.items.reduce((total, item) => {
                        return total + (this.normalizeCartQuantity(item.quantity) ?? 0);
                    }, 0);
                },

                normalizeCart(cart) {
                    if (! cart?.items) {
                        return cart;
                    }

                    if (cart.gift_notice?.message) {
                        this.$emitter.emit('add-flash', {
                            type: 'warning',
                            message: cart.gift_notice.message,
                        });

                        cart.gift_notice = null;
                    }

                    cart.items_qty = parseInt(cart.items_qty ?? 0);
                    cart.items_count = parseInt(cart.items_count ?? 0);

                    cart.items = cart.items.map(item => ({
                        ...item,
                        stock_qty: this.normalizeItemStockQty(item),
                        maxValue: item.maxValue ?? item.product?.maxValue ?? 1,
                    }));

                    return cart;
                },

                getItemStockQty(item) {
                    return this.normalizeItemStockQty(item);
                },

                normalizeItemStockQty(item) {
                    const value = item?.stock_qty ?? item?.maxValue ?? item?.product?.maxValue ?? item?.product?.qty ?? 0;
                    const stockQty = Number(value);

                    return Number.isFinite(stockQty) ? Math.max(0, Math.trunc(stockQty)) : 0;
                },

                getItemExtraPrices(item) {
    const prices = item?.extra_prices ?? item?.product?.extra_prices ?? [];

    return Array.isArray(prices) ? prices : [];
},

                getItemQuickQuantity(item) {
                    const quantities = this.getItemExtraPrices(item)
                        .map(price => Number(price?.qty ?? price?.quantity ?? price?.min_qty ?? 0))
                        .filter(quantity => Number.isInteger(quantity) && quantity > 0);

                    return quantities.length ? Math.min(...quantities) : null;
                },

                getItemQuickQuantityTarget(item) {
                    const quickQuantity = this.getItemQuickQuantity(item);

                    if (quickQuantity === null) {
                        return null;
                    }

                    const currentQuantity = Math.max(this.getItemQuantity(item), 1);
                    const targetQuantity = currentQuantity === 1
                        ? quickQuantity
                        : currentQuantity + quickQuantity;

                    return targetQuantity <= this.getItemStockQty(item)
                        ? targetQuantity
                        : null;
                },

                canAddItemQuickQuantity(item) {
                    return this.getItemQuickQuantityTarget(item) !== null;
                },

                addItemQuickQuantity(item) {
                    const targetQuantity = this.getItemQuickQuantityTarget(item);

                    if (targetQuantity === null) {
                        return;
                    }

                    this.updateItem(targetQuantity, item);
                },

                getItemQuantity(item) {
                    const quantity = Number(item?.quantity ?? 0);

                    return Number.isFinite(quantity) ? Math.max(0, Math.trunc(quantity)) : 0;
                },

                getActiveItemExtraPrice(item) {
                    const quantity = this.getItemQuantity(item);

                    return this.getItemExtraPrices(item)
                        .filter(price => {
                            const priceQuantity = Number(price?.qty ?? price?.quantity ?? price?.min_qty ?? 0);

                            return Number.isFinite(priceQuantity)
                                && priceQuantity > 0
                                && quantity >= priceQuantity;
                        })
                        .sort((a, b) => {
                            const aQty = Number(a?.qty ?? a?.quantity ?? a?.min_qty ?? 0);
                            const bQty = Number(b?.qty ?? b?.quantity ?? b?.min_qty ?? 0);

                            return bQty - aQty;
                        })[0] ?? null;
                },

                hasActiveItemExtraPrice(item) {
    return !! this.getActiveItemExtraPrice(item);
},

                shouldShowRegularItemTotal(item) {
                    if (! this.hasActiveItemExtraPrice(item)) {
                        return false;
                    }

    const regularTotal = Number(item?.regular_total ?? 0);
    const currentTotal = Number(item?.total ?? 0);

    return regularTotal > 0
        && currentTotal > 0
        && regularTotal !== currentTotal
        && !! item?.formatted_regular_total;
},
            },
        });
    </script>
    <script
        type="text/x-template"
        id="v-mobile-bottom-nav-template"
    >
        <nav
    class="fixed inset-x-0 bottom-0 z-[1001] border-t border-zinc-200 bg-white/80 px-2 pb-[max(0.375rem,env(safe-area-inset-bottom))] pt-1 shadow-[0_-8px_24px_rgba(0,0,0,0.08)] backdrop-blur lg:hidden"
    aria-label="shop::app.components.layouts.header.mobile-bottom-navigation"
>
    <div class="grid grid-cols-4 items-center">
        <a
            href="https://yumi.market"
            class="flex min-h-[52px] flex-col items-center justify-center gap-0.5 text-[11px] font-medium transition-colors"
            :class="isHomePage && ! activePanel ? 'border-b-2 border-navyBlue text-navyBlue' : 'text-gray-600 hover:text-navyBlue'"
            aria-label="Головна"
            :aria-current="isHomePage && ! activePanel ? 'page' : null"
        >
             <svg
                class="h-6 w-6"
                viewBox="0 0 24 24"
                fill="none"
                aria-hidden="true"
            >
                <path
                    d="M4 10.75L12 4l8 6.75V20a1 1 0 0 1-1 1h-4.5v-6h-5v6H5a1 1 0 0 1-1-1v-9.25Z"
                    stroke="currentColor"
                    stroke-width="1.8"
                    stroke-linecap="round"
                    stroke-linejoin="round"
                />
            </svg>

            <span>Головна</span>
        </a>

        <button
            type="button"
            class="flex min-h-[52px] flex-col items-center justify-center gap-0.5 text-[11px] font-medium transition-colors"
            :class="isCatalogOpen || (isCatalogPage && ! activePanel) ? 'border-b-2 border-navyBlue text-navyBlue' : 'text-gray-600 hover:text-navyBlue'"
            aria-label="Каталог"
            aria-controls="mobile-catalog-drawer"
            :aria-current="isCatalogPage && ! activePanel ? 'page' : null"
            :aria-expanded="isCatalogOpen"
            @click="toggleCatalog"
        >
            <span class="icon-hamburger text-2xl" aria-hidden="true"></span>
            <span>Каталог</span>
        </button>

                    <button
                type="button"
                class="flex min-h-[52px] flex-col items-center justify-center gap-0.5 text-[11px] font-medium transition-colors"
                :class="isMiniCartOpen || (isCartPage && ! activePanel) ? 'border-b-2 border-navyBlue text-navyBlue' : 'text-gray-600 hover:text-navyBlue'"
                aria-label="Кошик"
                aria-controls="mini-cart-drawer"
                :aria-current="isCartPage && ! activePanel ? 'page' : null"
                :aria-expanded="isMiniCartOpen"
                @click="toggleMiniCart"
            >
                    <span class="relative">
                <span class="icon-cart text-2xl" aria-hidden="true"></span>

                                    <span
                        class="absolute -top-2 rounded-[30px] bg-black px-[6px] py-[4px] text-xxs font-semibold leading-[9px] text-white ltr:left-5 rtl:right-5"
                        v-if="cart?.items_count"
                    >
                        {{ cart.items_count }}
                    </span>
                            </span>

            <span>Кошик</span>
                    </button>
        
                    <a
                href="https://yumi.market/customer/login"
                class="flex min-h-[52px] flex-col items-center justify-center gap-0.5 text-[11px] font-medium transition-colors"
                :class="isAccountPage && ! activePanel ? 'border-b-2 border-navyBlue text-navyBlue' : 'text-gray-600 hover:text-navyBlue'"
                aria-label="Кабінет"
                :aria-current="isAccountPage && ! activePanel ? 'page' : null"
            >
                <span class="icon-users text-2xl" aria-hidden="true"></span>
                <span>Кабінет</span>
            </a>
        
            </div>
</nav>
    </script>

    <script type="module">
        app.component('v-mobile-bottom-nav', {
            template: '#v-mobile-bottom-nav-template',

            data() {
                return {
                    activePanel: null,
                    isHomePage: false,
                    isCatalogPage: true,
                    isCartPage: false,
                    isAccountPage: false,
                    cart: null,
                };
            },

            mounted() {
                this.$emitter.on('update-mini-cart', this.updateCart);
                this.$emitter.on('open-mobile-catalog', this.onCatalogOpen);
                this.$emitter.on('close-mobile-catalog', this.onCatalogClose);
                this.$emitter.on('mini-cart-opened', this.onMiniCartOpen);
                this.$emitter.on('mini-cart-closed', this.onMiniCartClose);
            },

            beforeUnmount() {
                this.$emitter.off('update-mini-cart', this.updateCart);
                this.$emitter.off('open-mobile-catalog', this.onCatalogOpen);
                this.$emitter.off('close-mobile-catalog', this.onCatalogClose);
                this.$emitter.off('mini-cart-opened', this.onMiniCartOpen);
                this.$emitter.off('mini-cart-closed', this.onMiniCartClose);
            },

            computed: {
                isCatalogOpen() {
                    return this.activePanel === 'catalog';
                },

                isMiniCartOpen() {
                    return this.activePanel === 'cart';
                },
            },

            methods: {
                updateCart(cart) {
                    this.cart = cart;
                },

                onCatalogOpen() {
                    this.activePanel = 'catalog';
                },

                onCatalogClose() {
                    if (this.isCatalogOpen) {
                        this.activePanel = null;
                    }
                },

                onMiniCartOpen() {
                    this.activePanel = 'cart';
                },

                onMiniCartClose() {
                    if (this.isMiniCartOpen) {
                        this.activePanel = null;
                    }
                },

                toggleCatalog() {
                    const catalogDrawer = this.$root.$refs.mobileCatalogDrawer;

                    if (this.isCatalogOpen) {
                        catalogDrawer?.close();

                        return;
                    }

                    this.$emitter.emit('close-mini-cart');
                    catalogDrawer?.open();
                },

                toggleMiniCart() {
                    const catalogDrawer = this.$root.$refs.mobileCatalogDrawer;

                    if (this.isMiniCartOpen) {
                        this.$emitter.emit('close-mini-cart');

                        return;
                    }

                    catalogDrawer?.close();
                    this.$emitter.emit('open-mini-cart');
                },
            },
        });
    </script>

    <script type="module">
        app.component('v-products-carousel', {
            template: '#v-products-carousel-template',

            mixins: [window.yumiProductSliderMixin],

            props: {
                src: String,
                title: String,
                navigationLink: String,
                headingClass: {
                    type: String,
                    default: 'text-4xl max-md:text-2xl max-sm:text-xl',
                },
                sectionClass: {
                    type: String,
                    default: '',
                },
                compactCard: {
                    type: Boolean,
                    default: false,
                },
            },

            data() {
                return {
                    isLoading: true,

                    products: [],
                };
            },

            mounted() {
                this.getProducts();
            },

            methods: {
                getProducts() {
                    this.$axios.get(this.src)
                        .then(response => {
                            this.products = response.data.data ?? [];
                        }).catch(() => {
                            this.products = [];
                        }).finally(() => {
                            this.isLoading = false;
                        });
                },
            },
        });
    </script>

    <script type="module">
        app.component('v-product-gallery', {
            template: '#v-product-gallery-template',

            data() {

                return {
                    placements: {"top_left":[{"name":"\u041a\u0430\u0440\u0442\u043e\u0447\u043a\u0430 \u0442\u043e\u0432\u0430\u0440\u0430 \u041b\u0412","value":"\u0421\u0430\u0442\u0438\u043d","icon":null,"is_visible_icons_on_card":false}],"top_right":[{"name":"\u0420\u043e\u0437\u043c\u0456\u0440","value":"11\u0022 | 28 \u0441\u043c","icon":null,"is_visible_icons_on_card":false}],"bottom_left":[{"name":"\u0412\u0438\u0440\u043e\u0431\u043d\u0438\u043a","value":"Kalisan | \u0422\u0443\u0440\u0435\u0447\u0447\u0438\u043d\u0430","icon":"https:\/\/yumi.market\/storage\/attribute_option\/IJOdrjrSAypgfNcpGCMUXrPkIazLwzZuHqr3ipl9.svg","is_visible_icons_on_card":true}],"bottom_right":[]},

                    isImageZooming: false,

                    isMediaLoading: true,


                    media: {
                        images: [{"small_image_url":"https:\/\/yumi.market\/cache\/small\/product\/220\/EOlSRCM8iWdGghyqrujp15dkQ1fCgU9OaJG93M9V.webp","medium_image_url":"https:\/\/yumi.market\/cache\/medium\/product\/220\/EOlSRCM8iWdGghyqrujp15dkQ1fCgU9OaJG93M9V.webp","large_image_url":"https:\/\/yumi.market\/cache\/large\/product\/220\/EOlSRCM8iWdGghyqrujp15dkQ1fCgU9OaJG93M9V.webp","original_image_url":"https:\/\/yumi.market\/cache\/original\/product\/220\/EOlSRCM8iWdGghyqrujp15dkQ1fCgU9OaJG93M9V.webp"},{"small_image_url":"https:\/\/yumi.market\/cache\/small\/product\/220\/aLUUTRL8j0fHPpLby9OdhPhhvBkddkO2zLGupheA.webp","medium_image_url":"https:\/\/yumi.market\/cache\/medium\/product\/220\/aLUUTRL8j0fHPpLby9OdhPhhvBkddkO2zLGupheA.webp","large_image_url":"https:\/\/yumi.market\/cache\/large\/product\/220\/aLUUTRL8j0fHPpLby9OdhPhhvBkddkO2zLGupheA.webp","original_image_url":"https:\/\/yumi.market\/cache\/original\/product\/220\/aLUUTRL8j0fHPpLby9OdhPhhvBkddkO2zLGupheA.webp"},{"small_image_url":"https:\/\/yumi.market\/cache\/small\/product\/220\/EFCAED2bLCJ4haNInvW5f6ydUIRRqWPZhBmClMSr.webp","medium_image_url":"https:\/\/yumi.market\/cache\/medium\/product\/220\/EFCAED2bLCJ4haNInvW5f6ydUIRRqWPZhBmClMSr.webp","large_image_url":"https:\/\/yumi.market\/cache\/large\/product\/220\/EFCAED2bLCJ4haNInvW5f6ydUIRRqWPZhBmClMSr.webp","original_image_url":"https:\/\/yumi.market\/cache\/original\/product\/220\/EFCAED2bLCJ4haNInvW5f6ydUIRRqWPZhBmClMSr.webp"}],

                        videos: [],
                    },

                    baseFile: {
                        type: '',

                        path: ''
                    },

                    activeIndex: 0,

                    containerOffset: 110,
                };
            },

            watch: {
                'media.images': {
                    deep: true,

                    handler(newImages, oldImages) {
                        let selectedImage = newImages?.[this.activeIndex];

                        if (JSON.stringify(newImages) !== JSON.stringify(oldImages) && selectedImage?.large_image_url) {
                            this.baseFile.path = selectedImage.large_image_url;
                        }
                    },
                },
            },

            mounted() {
                if (this.media.images.length) {

                    this.baseFile.type = 'image';

                    this.baseFile.path = this.media.images[0].large_image_url;
                } else if (this.media.videos.length) {

                    this.baseFile.type = 'video';

                    this.baseFile.path = this.media.videos[0].video_url;
                }
            },

            computed: {
                isFirstImageActive() {
                    return this.activeIndex === 0
                        && this.baseFile.type === 'image';
                },

                allMedia() {
                    return [...this.media.images, ...this.media.videos];
                },

                mediaCount() {
                    return this.allMedia.length;
                },

                hasMultipleMedia() {
                    return this.mediaCount > 1;
                },

                hasThumbnailOverflow() {
                    return this.mediaCount > 5;
                },

                attachments() {
                    return [...this.media.images, ...this.media.videos].map(media => ({
                        url: media.type === 'videos' ? media.video_url : media.original_image_url,

                        type: media.type === 'videos' ? 'video' : 'image',
                    }));
                },
            },

            methods: {
                isActiveMedia(index) {
                    return index === this.activeIndex;
                },

                onMediaLoad() {
                    this.isMediaLoading = false;
                },

                change(media, index) {
                    this.isMediaLoading = true;

                    if (media.type == 'videos') {
                        this.baseFile.type = 'video';

                        this.baseFile.path = media.video_url;

                        this.onMediaLoad();
                    } else {
                        this.baseFile.type = 'image';

                        this.baseFile.path = media.large_image_url;
                    }

                    this.activeIndex = index;
                },

                swipePrevious() {
                    const container = this.$refs.swiperContainer;

                    container.scrollTop -= this.containerOffset;
                },

                swipeNext() {
                    const container = this.$refs.swiperContainer;

                    container.scrollTop += this.containerOffset;
                },
            },
        });
    </script>
        <script>
            function shareProduct() {
                let productName = "KALISAN-КЛ 11&quot; 14 Сатин Сніжно-білий | Opaque Satin Snow White";
                let productUrl = "https:\/\/yumi.market\/kalisan-kl-11-14-satin-snizno-bilii-opaque-satin-snow-white?locale=uk\u0026currency=UAH";

                if (navigator.share) {
                    navigator.share({
                        title: productName,
                        text: productName + ' ' + productUrl,
                        url: productUrl
                    })
                    .catch((error) => console.error('Error sharing:', error));
                } else {
                    alert('Your browser does not support sharing.');
                }
            }
        </script>
        <script
        type="text/x-template"
        id="v-accordion-template"
    >
        <div>
            <slot
                name="header"
                :toggle="toggle"
                :isOpen="isOpen"
            >
                admin::app.components.accordion.default-content            </slot>

            <transition
                @before-enter="beforeEnter"
                @enter="enter"
                @after-enter="afterEnter"
                @before-leave="beforeLeave"
                @leave="leave"
                @after-leave="afterLeave"
            >
                <div
                    v-show="isOpen"
                    class="overflow-hidden transition-[height,opacity] duration-300 ease-in-out"
                >
                    <slot
                        name="content"
                        :isOpen="isOpen"
                    >
                        admin::app.components.accordion.default-content                    </slot>
                </div>
            </transition>
        </div>
    </script>

    <script type="module">
        app.component('v-accordion', {
            template: '#v-accordion-template',

            props: {
                isActive: {
                    type: Boolean,
                    default: true,
                },
            },

            data() {
                return {
                    isOpen: this.isActive,
                };
            },

            methods: {
                toggle() {
                    this.isOpen = ! this.isOpen;

                    this.$emit('toggle', { isActive: this.isOpen });
                },

                beforeEnter(el) {
                    el.style.height = '0';
                    el.style.opacity = '0';
                },

                enter(el) {
                    el.style.overflow = 'hidden';

                    requestAnimationFrame(() => {
                        el.style.height = `${el.scrollHeight}px`;
                        el.style.opacity = '1';
                    });
                },

                afterEnter(el) {
                    el.style.height = 'auto';
                    el.style.overflow = 'visible';
                },

                beforeLeave(el) {
                    el.style.height = `${el.scrollHeight}px`;
                    el.style.opacity = '1';
                    el.style.overflow = 'hidden';
                },

                leave(el) {
                    void el.offsetHeight;

                    requestAnimationFrame(() => {
                        el.style.height = '0';
                        el.style.opacity = '0';
                    });
                },

                afterLeave(el) {
                    el.style.height = '0';
                    el.style.opacity = '0';
                    el.style.overflow = 'hidden';
                },
            },
        });
    </script>

    <script type="module">
        app.component('v-yumi-contact-links', {
            template: '#v-yumi-contact-links-template',

            props: {
                listClass: {
                    type: String,
                    default: '',
                },

                linkClass: {
                    type: String,
                    default: '',
                },

                iconClass: {
                    type: String,
                    default: '',
                },
            },
            data() {
                return {
                    activeModal: null,
                    previousDocumentOverflow: '',
                };
            },

            methods: {
                open(modal) {
                    this.activeModal = modal;
                    this.previousDocumentOverflow = document.documentElement.style.overflow;
                    document.documentElement.style.overflow = 'hidden';
                },

                close() {
                    this.activeModal = null;
                    document.documentElement.style.overflow = this.previousDocumentOverflow;
                },
            },
        });
    </script>
    <script
        type="text/x-template"
        id="v-date-picker-template"
    >
        <span class="relative">
            <slot></slot>

            <i class="icon-calendar pointer-events-none absolute top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-2xl text-gray-400 ltr:right-2 rtl:left-2"></i>
        </span>
    </script>

    <script type="module">
        app.component('v-date-picker', {
            template: '#v-date-picker-template',

            props: {
                name: String,

                value: String,

                allowInput: {
                    type: Boolean,
                    default: true,
                },

                disable: Array,

                minDate: String,

                maxDate: String,
            },

            data: function() {
                return {
                    datepicker: null
                };
            },

            mounted: function() {
                let options = this.setOptions();

                this.activate(options);
            },

            methods: {
                setOptions: function() {
                    let self = this;

                    return {
                        allowInput: this.allowInput ?? true,
                        disable: this.disable ?? [],
                        minDate: this.minDate ?? '',
                        maxDate: this.maxDate ?? '',
                        altFormat: "Y-m-d",
                        dateFormat: "Y-m-d",
                        weekNumbers: true,

                        onChange: function(selectedDates, dateStr, instance) {
                            self.$emit("onChange", dateStr);
                        }
                    };
                },

                activate: function(options) {
                    let element = this.$el.getElementsByTagName("input")[0];

                    this.datepicker = new Flatpickr(element, options);
                },

                clear: function() {
                    this.datepicker.clear();
                }
            }
        });
    </script>
    <script
        type="text/x-template"
        id="v-datetime-picker-template"
    >
        <span class="relative">
            <slot></slot>

            <i class="icon-calendar pointer-events-none absolute top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-2xl text-gray-400 ltr:right-2 rtl:left-2"></i>
        </span>
    </script>

    <script type="module">
        app.component('v-datetime-picker', {
            template: '#v-datetime-picker-template',

            props: {
                name: String,

                value: String,

                allowInput: {
                    type: Boolean,
                    default: true,
                },

                disable: Array,

                minDate: String,

                maxDate: String,
            },

            data: function() {
                return {
                    datepicker: null
                };
            },

            mounted: function() {
                let options = this.setOptions();

                this.activate(options);
            },

            methods: {
                setOptions: function() {
                    let self = this;

                    return {
                        allowInput: this.allowInput ?? true,
                        disable: this.disable ?? [],
                        minDate: this.minDate ?? '',
                        maxDate: this.maxDate ?? '',
                        altFormat: "Y-m-d H:i:S",
                        dateFormat: "Y-m-d H:i:S",
                        enableTime: true,
                        time_24hr: true,
                        weekNumbers: true,

                        onChange: function(selectedDates, dateStr, instance) {
                            self.$emit("onChange", dateStr);
                        }
                    };
                },

                activate: function(options) {
                    let element = this.$el.getElementsByTagName("input")[0];

                    this.datepicker = new Flatpickr(element, options);
                },

                clear: function() {
                    this.datepicker.clear();
                }
            }
        });
    </script>
    <script
        type="text/x-template"
        id="v-dropdown-template"
    >
        <div>
            <div
                class="select-none"
                ref="toggleBlock"
                @click="toggle()"
            >
                <slot name="toggle">Toggle</slot>
            </div>

            <transition
                tag="div"
                name="dropdown"
                enter-active-class="transition duration-100 ease-out"
                enter-from-class="scale-95 transform opacity-0"
                enter-to-class="scale-100 transform opacity-100"
                leave-active-class="transition duration-75 ease-in"
                leave-from-class="scale-100 transform opacity-100"
                leave-to-class="scale-95 transform opacity-0"
            >
                <div
                    class="absolute z-50 w-max overflow-hidden rounded-[20px] bg-white shadow-[0px_10px_84px_rgba(0,0,0,0.1)] max-md:rounded-lg"
                    :style="positionStyles"
                    v-show="isActive"
                >
                    <slot name="content"></slot>

                    <slot name="menu"></slot>
                </div>
            </transition>
        </div>
    </script>

    <script type="module">
        app.component('v-dropdown', {
            template: '#v-dropdown-template',

            props: {
                position: String,

                closeOnClick: {
                    type: Boolean,
                    required: false,
                    default: true
                },
            },

            data() {
                return {
                    toggleBlockWidth: 0,

                    toggleBlockHeight: 0,

                    isActive: false,
                };
            },

            created() {
                window.addEventListener('click', this.handleFocusOut);
            },

            mounted() {
                this.toggleBlockWidth = this.$refs.toggleBlock.clientWidth;

                this.toggleBlockHeight = this.$refs.toggleBlock.clientHeight;
            },

            beforeDestroy() {
                window.removeEventListener('click', this.handleFocusOut);
            },

            computed: {
                positionStyles() {
                    switch (this.position) {
                        case 'bottom-left':
                            return [
                                `min-width: ${this.toggleBlockWidth}px`,
                                `top: ${this.toggleBlockHeight}px`,
                                'left: 0',
                            ];

                        case 'bottom-right':
                            return [
                                `min-width: ${this.toggleBlockWidth}px`,
                                `top: ${this.toggleBlockHeight}px`,
                                'right: 0',
                            ];

                        case 'top-left':
                            return [
                                `min-width: ${this.toggleBlockWidth}px`,
                                `bottom: ${this.toggleBlockHeight}px`,
                                'left: 0',
                            ];

                        case 'top-right':
                            return [
                                `min-width: ${this.toggleBlockWidth}px`,
                                `bottom: ${this.toggleBlockHeight}px`,
                                'right: 0',
                            ];

                        default:
                            return [
                                `min-width: ${this.toggleBlockWidth}px`,
                                `top: ${this.toggleBlockHeight}px`,
                                'left: 0',
                            ];
                    }
                },
            },

            methods: {
                toggle() {
                    /**
                     * If still somehow width is zero then this will check for width one more time.
                     */
                    if (this.toggleBlockWidth === 0) {
                        this.toggleBlockWidth = this.$refs.toggleBlock.clientWidth;
                    }

                    /**
                     * If still somehow height is zero then this will check for height one more time.
                     */
                    if (this.toggleBlockHeight === 0) {
                        this.toggleBlockHeight = this.$refs.toggleBlock.clientHeight;
                    }

                    this.isActive = ! this.isActive;
                },

                handleFocusOut(e) {
                    if (! this.$el.contains(e.target) || (this.closeOnClick && this.$el.children[1].contains(e.target))) {
                        this.isActive = false;
                    }
                },
            },
        });
    </script>
        <script
            type="text/x-template"
            id="v-desktop-category-template"
        >
        <div class="relative">
            <!-- Default category layout -->
            <div
                class="flex items-center"
                v-if="'sidebar' !== 'sidebar'"
            >



                <div
                    class="main-navi-button font-medium group relative flex min-h-[50px] items-center border-b-4 border-transparent hover:border-b-4 hover:border-navyBlue"
                    v-for="category in categories"
                >

                        <a
                            :href="category.url"
                            class="block flex-1 px-5 h-full uppercase"
                        >
                           {{ category.name }}
                        </a>


                 <div
  class="pointer-events-none absolute top-[78px] z-[1]
