        <script
            type="text/x-template"
            id="v-search-suggestions-template"
        >
    <div
        class="relative w-full"
        ref="root"
    >
        <form
            ref="form"
            :action="searchAction"
            class="flex max-w-[445px] items-center"
            role="search"
            autocomplete="off"
            @submit.prevent="submitSearch"
        >
            <label
                for="organic-search"
                class="sr-only"
            >
                Пошук            </label>

            <div class="icon-search pointer-events-none absolute top-2.5 flex items-center text-xl ltr:left-3 rtl:right-3"></div>

            <input
                id="organic-search"
                ref="input"
                type="text"
                name="query"
                v-model="query"
                class="block w-full rounded-lg border border-1 bg-white/90 px-11 py-3 text-xs font-medium text-gray-900 transition-all hover:border-gray-400 focus:border-gray-400"
                :maxlength="maxLength"
                placeholder="Шукати товари тут"
                role="combobox"
                 aria-controls="organic-search-results"

                aria-label="Шукати товари тут"
                :aria-expanded="isOpen ? 'true' : 'false'"
                aria-autocomplete="list"
                pattern="[^\\]+"
                @input="onInput"
                @focus="onFocus"
                @keydown.escape="closeDropdown"
            >

            <button
                type="submit"
                class="hidden"
                aria-label="Надіслати"
            >
            </button>

            <slot></slot>
        </form>

        <div
    v-if="isOpen && suggestions.length"
    id="organic-search-results"
    role="listbox"
    class="absolute top-full z-50 mt-2 w-full max-w-[445px] overflow-hidden rounded-lg border border-zinc-200 bg-white shadow-lg"
>
            <div class="border-b border-zinc-100 px-4 py-3 text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wide text-zinc-500">
                Часто шукають            </div>

            <div class="py-1">
               <a
    v-for="item in suggestions"
    role="option"
    :key="item.url"
    :href="item.url"
    class="block px-4 py-3 text-sm text-gray-800 transition hover:bg-zinc-50"
    @click="closeDropdown"
>
                    {{ item.term }}
                </a>
    </div>
        </div>
    </div>
</script>

        <script type="module">
        app.component('v-search-suggestions', {
    template: '#v-search-suggestions-template',

    props: {
        initialQuery: {
            type: String,
            default: '',
        },

        searchAction: {
            type: String,
            required: true,
        },

        suggestionsUrl: {
            type: String,
            required: true,
        },

        minLength: {
            type: Number,
            default: 2,
        },

        maxLength: {
            type: Number,
            default: 255,
        },
    },

    data() {
        return {
            query: this.initialQuery,
            suggestions: [],
            isOpen: false,
            isLoading: false,
            debounceTimer: null,
            abortController: null,
        };
    },

    mounted() {
        document.addEventListener('click', this.handleClickOutside);
    },

    beforeUnmount() {
        document.removeEventListener('click', this.handleClickOutside);

        if (this.debounceTimer) {
            clearTimeout(this.debounceTimer);
        }

        if (this.abortController) {
            this.abortController.abort();
        }
    },

    methods: {

    submitSearch() {
        const value = (this.query || '').trim();

        this.closeDropdown();

        if (! value) {
            window.location.href = "https://yumi.market";
            return;
        }

        this.query = value;

        this.$nextTick(() => {
            this.$refs.form?.submit();
        });
    },
        onInput() {
            if (this.debounceTimer) {
                clearTimeout(this.debounceTimer);
            }

            const value = this.query.trim();

            if (value.length < this.minLength) {
                this.suggestions = [];
                this.closeDropdown();

                return;
            }

            this.debounceTimer = setTimeout(() => {
                this.fetchSuggestions(value);
            }, 250);
        },

        onFocus() {
            if (this.suggestions.length) {
                this.isOpen = true;
            }
        },

        closeDropdown() {
            this.isOpen = false;
        },

        handleClickOutside(event) {
            if (! this.$refs.root?.contains(event.target)) {
                this.closeDropdown();
            }
        },

        async fetchSuggestions(value) {
            if (this.abortController) {
                this.abortController.abort();
            }

            this.abortController = new AbortController();
            this.isLoading = true;

            try {
                const response = await this.$axios.get(this.suggestionsUrl, {
                    params: {
                        q: value,
                    },
                    signal: this.abortController.signal,
                    headers: {
                        'X-Requested-With': 'XMLHttpRequest',
                        'Accept': 'application/json',
                    },
                });

                this.suggestions = Array.isArray(response.data?.data)
                    ? response.data.data
                    : [];

                this.isOpen = this.suggestions.length > 0;
            } catch (error) {
                if (
                    error?.name !== 'CanceledError'
                    && error?.name !== 'AbortError'
                ) {
                    this.suggestions = [];
                    this.closeDropdown();
                }
            } finally {
                this.isLoading = false;
            }
        },
    },
});

            app.component('v-desktop-category', {
                template: '#v-desktop-category-template',

                data() {
                    return {
                        categories: [],
                        topMenuCategories: [{"id":910,"name":"\u0410\u043a\u0446\u0456\u044f","url":"\/akciini-tovari-promo"},{"id":915,"name":"\u041d\u043e\u0432\u0438\u043d\u043a\u0438","url":"\/novynky-30"},{"id":1003,"name":"Final Sale","url":"\/rozprodaj"}],
                        hoveredCategory: null,
                        hoveredSecondLevelCategory: null,
                        hoveredThirdLevelCategory: null,
                        activeSecondLevelCategory: null,
                        hoverTimer: null,
                        hoverDelay: 180,
                        secondLevelHoverTimer: null,
                        isDrawerActive: false,
                        scrollFades: {
                            first: { top: false, bottom: false },
                            second: { top: false, bottom: false },
                            third: { top: false, bottom: false },
                        },
                    };
                },
    computed: {
        topNavigationCategories() {
    const source = this.topMenuCategories.length
        ? this.topMenuCategories
        : this.categories.filter(category => category.visible_in_top_menu);

    return source.map(category => {
        const fullCategory = this.categories.find(item => item.id == category.id);

        return {
            ...category,
            icon_url: fullCategory?.icon_url ?? category.icon_url ?? null,
        };
    });
},

        visibleSecondLevelCategories() {
            return (this.hoveredCategory?.children || [])
                .filter(category => ! category.hide_in_desktop_burger_menu);
        },

        visibleThirdLevelCategories() {
            return this.visibleThirdLevelCategoriesFor(this.activeSecondLevelCategory);
        },

        activeBannerCategory() {
            if (this.hoveredThirdLevelCategory?.menu_banner_path) {
                return this.hoveredThirdLevelCategory;
            }

            if (this.hoveredSecondLevelCategory?.menu_banner_path) {
                return this.hoveredSecondLevelCategory;
            }

            return this.hoveredCategory;
        },

    },


   mounted() {
    this.ensureCategoriesLoaded();
},

    beforeUnmount() {
        this.resetDrawerState();
    },




    methods: {
        // Фарбування назви каталогу у колір стікерів
        menuTitleColorClass(category) {
            const colors = {
                top100: 'text-amber-600',
                new: 'text-lime-600',
                final: 'text-neutral-900',
                sale: 'text-red-700',
                exclusive: 'text-exclusive',
            };

            return colors[category?.menu_title_color] || 'text-black';
        },
        highlightNew(name) {
            const escaped = String(name ?? '')
                .replace(/&/g, '&amp;')
                .replace(/</g, '&lt;')
                .replace(/>/g, '&gt;')
                .replace(/"/g, '&quot;')
                .replace(/'/g, '&#039;');

            return escaped.replace(/\bNEW\b/gu, '<span class="text-lime-600 font-semibold">NEW</span>');
        },

        visibleThirdLevelCategoriesFor(category) {
            return (category?.children || [])
                .filter(child => ! child.hide_in_desktop_burger_menu);
        },

        setDefaultSecondLevelCategory(category = this.hoveredCategory) {
            this.hoveredThirdLevelCategory = null;
            this.activeSecondLevelCategory = (category?.children || [])
                .filter(child => ! child.hide_in_desktop_burger_menu)
                .find(child => this.visibleThirdLevelCategoriesFor(child).length)
                ?? null;

            this.recalcScrollArrow(['second', 'third']);
        },

        clearSecondLevelHoverTimer() {
            clearTimeout(this.secondLevelHoverTimer);
            this.secondLevelHoverTimer = null;
        },

        onSecondLevelHover(category) {
            this.clearSecondLevelHoverTimer();
            this.hoveredThirdLevelCategory = null;

            this.secondLevelHoverTimer = setTimeout(() => {
                this.hoveredSecondLevelCategory = category;
                this.activateSecondLevelCategory(category);
            }, this.hoverDelay);
        },

        activateSecondLevelCategory(category) {
            this.clearSecondLevelHoverTimer();
            this.hoveredThirdLevelCategory = null;
            this.activeSecondLevelCategory = this.visibleThirdLevelCategoriesFor(category).length
                ? category
                : null;

            this.recalcScrollArrow(['second', 'third']);
        },

        toggleSecondLevelCategory(category) {
            this.hoveredSecondLevelCategory = category;
            this.hoveredThirdLevelCategory = null;

            if (this.activeSecondLevelCategory?.id === category.id) {
                this.activeSecondLevelCategory = null;
                return;
            }

            this.activateSecondLevelCategory(category);
        },

        activateThirdLevelCategory(category) {
            this.hoveredThirdLevelCategory = category;
        },

        recalcScrollArrow(levels = ['first', 'second', 'third']) {
            this.$nextTick(() => {
                requestAnimationFrame(() => {
                    levels.forEach(level => {
                        this.checkScroll(this.getScrollContainer(level), level);
                    });
                });
            });
        },

        getScrollContainer(level = 'first') {
            const refs = {
                first: 'desktopFirstLevelContainer',
                second: 'desktopSecondLevelContainer',
                third: 'desktopThirdLevelContainer',
            };

            return this.$refs[refs[level]] || null;
        },

        onLevelScroll(level, event) {
            this.checkScroll(event.currentTarget, level);
        },

        onLevelOverlayWheel(level, event) {
            const container = this.getScrollContainer(level);

            if (! container || container.contains(event.target)) {
                return;
            }

            container.scrollTop += event.deltaY;
        },

        checkScroll(container, level = 'first') {
            if (!container) {
                this.scrollFades[level].top = false;
                this.scrollFades[level].bottom = false;

                return;
            }

            const canScrollUp = container.scrollTop > 5;
            const canScrollDown =
                container.scrollTop + container.clientHeight < container.scrollHeight - 5;

            this.scrollFades[level].top = canScrollUp;
            this.scrollFades[level].bottom = canScrollDown;
        },

        scrollToBottom(level = 'first') {
            const container = this.getScrollContainer(level);

            if (!container) return;

            container.scrollTop = Math.max(0, container.scrollHeight - container.clientHeight);
            this.checkScroll(container, level);
        },

        setDefaultHoveredCategory() {
            if (this.hoveredCategory) {
                if (! this.activeSecondLevelCategory) {
                    this.setDefaultSecondLevelCategory();
                }

                return;
            }

    this.hoveredCategory =
        this.categories.find(c => c.children && c.children.length && !c.url.includes('/search'))
        ?? null;

    if (this.hoveredCategory) {
        this.setDefaultSecondLevelCategory();
    }
},

        onCategoryHover(category) {
        clearTimeout(this.hoverTimer);

        this.hoverTimer = setTimeout(() => {
            this.hoveredCategory = category;
            this.hoveredSecondLevelCategory = null;
            this.hoveredThirdLevelCategory = null;
            this.setDefaultSecondLevelCategory(category);
        }, this.hoverDelay);
    },

        onCategoryLeave() {
            clearTimeout(this.hoverTimer);
        },

        ensureCategoriesLoaded() {
            if (this.categories.length) {
                return Promise.resolve(this.categories);
            }

            return window.yumiCategoryTreeLoader.load(this.$axios)
                .then(categories => {
                    this.categories = categories;
                    this.hoveredCategory = null;
                    this.hoveredSecondLevelCategory = null;
                    this.hoveredThirdLevelCategory = null;
                    this.activeSecondLevelCategory = null;

                    if (this.isDrawerActive) {
                        this.recalcScrollArrow();
                    }

                    return categories;
                })
                .catch(() => {
                    this.categories = [];

                    return [];
                });
        },

        onDrawerToggle(event) {
            this.isDrawerActive = event.isActive;

            if (! this.isDrawerActive) {
                this.resetDrawerState();
                return;
            }

            this.ensureCategoriesLoaded().then(() => {
                if (! this.isDrawerActive) {
                    return;
                }

                this.setDefaultHoveredCategory();
                this.recalcScrollArrow();
            });
        },

        resetDrawerState() {
            this.hoveredCategory = null;
            this.hoveredSecondLevelCategory = null;
            this.hoveredThirdLevelCategory = null;
            this.activeSecondLevelCategory = null;
            this.isDrawerActive = false;
            clearTimeout(this.hoverTimer);
            this.clearSecondLevelHoverTimer();
            this.scrollFades.first = { top: false, bottom: false };
            this.scrollFades.second = { top: false, bottom: false };
            this.scrollFades.third = { top: false, bottom: false };
        },
    },
});
</script>
    <script
        type="text/x-template"
        id="v-drawer-template"
    >
        <div>
            <!-- Toggler -->
            <div v-if="hasToggle" @click="open">
                <slot name="toggle">

                </slot>
            </div>

            <!-- Overlay -->
            <transition
                tag="div"
                name="drawer-overlay"
                enter-class="duration-300 ease-out"
                enter-from-class="opacity-0"
                enter-to-class="opacity-100"
                leave-class="duration-200 ease-in"
                leave-from-class="opacity-100"
                leave-to-class="opacity-0"
            >
                <div
                    class="fixed inset-0 z-20 bg-gray-500 bg-opacity-50 transition-opacity"
                    v-show="isOpen"
                    @click="close"
                ></div>
            </transition>

            <!-- Content -->
            <transition
                tag="div"
                name="drawer"
                :enter-from-class="enterFromLeaveToClasses"
                enter-active-class="transform transition duration-200 ease-in-out"
                enter-to-class="translate-x-0"
                leave-from-class="translate-x-0"
                leave-active-class="transform transition duration-200 ease-in-out"
                :leave-to-class="enterFromLeaveToClasses"
            >
                <div
                    class="fixed z-[99999] overflow-hidden bg-white max-md:!w-full"
                    :class="{
                        'inset-x-0 top-0': position == 'top',
                        'inset-x-0 bottom-0 max-sm:max-h-full': position == 'bottom',
                        'inset-y-0 ltr:right-0 rtl:left-0': position == 'right',
                        'inset-y-0 ltr:left-0 rtl:right-0': position == 'left'
                    }"
                    :style="'width:' + width"
                    v-show="isOpen"
                    role="dialog"
                    aria-modal="true"
                >
                    <div class="pointer-events-auto h-full w-full overflow-hidden bg-white"
 @touchstart="handleTouchStart"
    @touchend="handleTouchEnd"
>
                        <div class="flex h-full min-h-0 w-full flex-col">
                            <slot
                                name="header"
                                :close="close"
                            >
                                Default Header
                            </slot>

                            <!-- Content Slot -->
                            <slot name="content"></slot>

                            <!-- Footer Slot -->
                            <slot name="footer"></slot>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                </div>
            </transition>
        </div>
    </script>

    <script type="module">
        app.component('v-drawer', {
            template: '#v-drawer-template',

            props: [
                'isActive',
                'position',
                'width',
                'hasToggle'
            ],

            data() {
                return {
                    isOpen: this.isActive,
                    touchStartX: 0,
                    touchStartY: 0,
                    touchEndX: 0,
                    touchEndY: 0,
                    scrollPosition: 0,
                    previousBodyStyles: {},
                    previousDocumentOverflow: '',
                    hasLockedBodyScroll: false,
                };
            },

            watch: {
                isActive: function(newVal, oldVal) {
                    this.isOpen = newVal;

                    if (newVal) {
                        this.lockBodyScroll();
                    } else {
                        this.unlockBodyScroll();
                    }
                }
            },

            computed: {
                enterFromLeaveToClasses() {
                    if (this.position == 'top') {
                        return '-translate-y-full';
                    } else if (this.position == 'bottom') {
                        return 'translate-y-full';
                    } else if (this.position == 'left') {
                        return 'ltr:-translate-x-full rtl:translate-x-full';
                    } else if (this.position == 'right') {
                        return 'ltr:translate-x-full rtl:-translate-x-full';
                    }
                }
            },

            methods: {
	handleTouchStart(e) {
        this.touchStartX = e.changedTouches[0].screenX;
        this.touchStartY = e.changedTouches[0].screenY;
    },

    handleTouchEnd(e) {
        this.touchEndX = e.changedTouches[0].screenX;
        this.touchEndY = e.changedTouches[0].screenY;
        this.handleSwipe();
    },

    handleSwipe() {
        const swipeThreshold = 50; // мінімальна відстань свайпу
        const horizontalDominanceRatio = 1.5;
        const deltaX = this.touchEndX - this.touchStartX;
        const deltaY = this.touchEndY - this.touchStartY;
        const isHorizontalSwipe = Math.abs(deltaX) > swipeThreshold
            && Math.abs(deltaX) > Math.abs(deltaY) * horizontalDominanceRatio;

        if (! isHorizontalSwipe) {
            return;
        }

        if (deltaX < 0 && this.position === 'left') {
            this.close({ forceScrollUnlock: true });
        }
        if (deltaX > 0 && this.position === 'right') {
            this.close({ forceScrollUnlock: true });
        }
    },
                getCurrentScrollPosition() {
                    const lockedBodyTop = Number.parseFloat(document.body.style.top || '');

                    if (
                        document.body.style.position === 'fixed'
                        && Number.isFinite(lockedBodyTop)
                        && lockedBodyTop < 0
                    ) {
                        return Math.abs(Math.round(lockedBodyTop));
                    }

                    return window.scrollY
                        || document.documentElement.scrollTop
                        || document.body.scrollTop
                        || 0;
                },

                getRestoreScrollPosition(lockState) {
                    const lockedScrollPosition = lockState?.scrollPosition;

                    if (lockedScrollPosition !== null && lockedScrollPosition !== undefined) {
                        const parsedLockedScrollPosition = Number(lockedScrollPosition);

                        if (Number.isFinite(parsedLockedScrollPosition)) {
                            return parsedLockedScrollPosition;
                        }
                    }

                    const lockedBodyTop = Number.parseFloat(document.body.style.top || '');

                    if (Number.isFinite(lockedBodyTop) && lockedBodyTop < 0) {
                        return Math.abs(Math.round(lockedBodyTop));
                    }

                    const instanceScrollPosition = Number(this.scrollPosition);

                    if (Number.isFinite(instanceScrollPosition)) {
                        return instanceScrollPosition;
                    }

                    return this.getCurrentScrollPosition();
                },

                toggle() {
                    this.isOpen = ! this.isOpen;

                    if (this.isOpen) {
                        this.lockBodyScroll();
                    } else {
                        this.unlockBodyScroll();
                    }

                    this.$emit('toggle', { isActive: this.isOpen });
                },

                open() {
                    if (this.isOpen) {
                        return;
                    }

                    this.isOpen = true;

                    this.lockBodyScroll();

                    this.$emit('open', { isActive: this.isOpen });
                },

                close(options = {}) {
                    if (! this.isOpen) {
                        return;
                    }

                    this.isOpen = false;

                    this.unlockBodyScroll(options.forceScrollUnlock);

                    this.$emit('close', { isActive: this.isOpen });
                },

                lockBodyScroll() {
                    if (this.hasLockedBodyScroll) {
                        return;
                    }

                    const lockState = window.yumiDrawerScrollLock || {
                        count: 0,
                        scrollPosition: 0,
                        previousDocumentOverflow: '',
                        previousBodyStyles: {},
                    };

                    window.yumiDrawerScrollLock = lockState;

                    if (lockState.count === 0) {
                        const scrollbarWidth = window.innerWidth - document.documentElement.clientWidth;

                        lockState.scrollPosition = this.getCurrentScrollPosition();
                        this.scrollPosition = lockState.scrollPosition;
                        lockState.previousDocumentOverflow = document.documentElement.style.overflow;
                        lockState.previousBodyStyles = {
                            overflow: document.body.style.overflow,
                            position: document.body.style.position,
                            top: document.body.style.top,
                            left: document.body.style.left,
                            right: document.body.style.right,
                            width: document.body.style.width,
                            paddingRight: document.body.style.paddingRight,
                        };

                        document.documentElement.style.overflow = 'hidden';

                        document.body.style.overflow = 'hidden';
                        document.body.style.position = 'fixed';
                        document.body.style.top = `-${lockState.scrollPosition}px`;
                        document.body.style.left = '0';
                        document.body.style.right = '0';
                        document.body.style.width = '100%';
                        document.body.style.paddingRight = scrollbarWidth ? `${scrollbarWidth}px` : '';
                    }

                    lockState.count += 1;
                    this.hasLockedBodyScroll = true;
                    document.body.dataset.drawerScrollLocked = 'true';
                },

                unlockBodyScroll(force = false) {
                    const lockState = window.yumiDrawerScrollLock;

                    if (! this.hasLockedBodyScroll && ! lockState) {
                        return;
                    }

                    if (lockState) {
                        lockState.count = force ? 0 : Math.max(lockState.count - 1, 0);
                    }

                    this.hasLockedBodyScroll = false;

                    if (lockState?.count > 0) {
                        return;
                    }

                    delete document.body.dataset.drawerScrollLocked;

                    document.documentElement.style.overflow = lockState?.previousDocumentOverflow || '';

                    const scrollPosition = this.getRestoreScrollPosition(lockState);

                    const previousBodyStyles = lockState?.previousBodyStyles || {
                        overflow: '',
                        position: '',
                        top: '',
                        left: '',
                        right: '',
                        width: '',
                        paddingRight: '',
                    };

                    Object.entries(previousBodyStyles).forEach(([property, value]) => {
                        document.body.style[property] = value;
                    });

                    window.yumiDrawerScrollLock = null;
                    window.scrollTo(0, scrollPosition);
                    window.requestAnimationFrame(() => window.scrollTo(0, scrollPosition));

                    if (force) {
                        window.setTimeout(() => {
                            if (window.yumiDrawerScrollLock) {
                                return;
                            }

                            document.documentElement.style.overflow = lockState?.previousDocumentOverflow || '';

                            Object.entries(previousBodyStyles).forEach(([property, value]) => {
                                document.body.style[property] = value;
                            });

                            window.scrollTo(0, scrollPosition);
                        }, 300);
                    }
                }
            },

            mounted() {
                this.handleEscape = (event) => {
                    if (event.key === 'Escape' && this.isOpen) {
                        this.close();
                    }
                };

                window.addEventListener('keydown', this.handleEscape);
            },

            beforeUnmount() {
                window.removeEventListener('keydown', this.handleEscape);

                if (this.isOpen) {
                    this.unlockBodyScroll();
                }
            },
        });
    </script>

        <script type="module">
            app.component('v-product', {
                template: '#v-product-template',

                data() {
                    return {
                        isWishlist: Boolean(""),

                        isCustomer: '',

                        is_buy_now: 0,

                        selectedQuantity: 1,

                        availableQuantity: 2668,

                        copiedLink: false,

                        isStoring: {
                            addToCart: false,

                            buyNow: false,

                            stockAlert: false,
                        },

                        stockAlertContact: "",
                        badges: [],
                        isSale: false,

                        shareTranslations: {
                            copy: "\u0421\u043a\u043e\u043f\u0456\u044e\u0432\u0430\u0442\u0438 \u043f\u043e\u0441\u0438\u043b\u0430\u043d\u043d\u044f",
                            copied: "\u041f\u043e\u0441\u0438\u043b\u0430\u043d\u043d\u044f \u0441\u043a\u043e\u043f\u0456\u0439\u043e\u0432\u0430\u043d\u043e",
                            failed: "\u041d\u0435 \u0432\u0434\u0430\u043b\u043e\u0441\u044f \u0441\u043a\u043e\u043f\u0456\u044e\u0432\u0430\u0442\u0438 \u043f\u043e\u0441\u0438\u043b\u0430\u043d\u043d\u044f",
                        },
                    }
                },

                methods: {
                   getQuickQuantityTarget(amount) {
                    const currentQty = Math.max(
                        Math.trunc(Number(this.selectedQuantity)) || 1,
                        1
                    );

                    const quickQty = Math.trunc(Number(amount));

                    if (! Number.isInteger(quickQty) || quickQty < 1) {
                        return null;
                    }

                    const targetQty = currentQty === 1
                        ? quickQty
                        : currentQty + quickQty;

                    return targetQty <= this.availableQuantity
                        ? targetQty
                        : null;
                },
                   canAddQuickQty(amount) {
                        return this.getQuickQuantityTarget(amount) !== null;
                   },

                    addQuickQty(amount) {
                        const targetQty = this.getQuickQuantityTarget(amount);

                        if (targetQty !== null) {
                            this.selectedQuantity = targetQty;
                        }
                    },

                    addToCart(params) {
                        const operation = this.is_buy_now ? 'buyNow' : 'addToCart';

                        this.isStoring[operation] = true;

                        let formData = new FormData(this.$refs.formData);

                        this.ensureQuantity(formData);

                        this.$axios.post('https://yumi.market/api/checkout/cart', formData, {
                                headers: {
                                    'Content-Type': 'multipart/form-data'
                                }
                            })
                            .then(response => {
                                if (response.data.message) {
                                    this.$emitter.emit('update-mini-cart', response.data.data);

                                    this.$emitter.emit('add-flash', { type: 'success', message: response.data.message });

                                    if (response.data.redirect) {
                                        window.location.href= response.data.redirect;
                                    }
                                } else {
                                    this.$emitter.emit('add-flash', { type: 'warning', message: response.data.data.message });
                                }

                                this.isStoring[operation] = false;
                            })
                            .catch(error => {
                                this.isStoring[operation] = false;

                                this.$emitter.emit('add-flash', { type: 'warning', message: error.response.data.message });
                            });
                    },

                    addToWishlist() {
                        if (this.isCustomer) {
                            this.$axios.post('https://yumi.market/api/customer/wishlist', {
                                    product_id: "220"
                                })
                                .then(response => {
                                    this.isWishlist = ! this.isWishlist;

                                    this.$emitter.emit('update-wishlist', {
                                        delta: this.isWishlist ? 1 : -1,
                                    });
                                    this.$emitter.emit('add-flash', { type: 'success', message: response.data.data.message });
                                })
                                .catch(error => {});
                        } else {
                            window.location.href = "https://yumi.market/customer/login";
                        }
                    },

                    addToCompare(productId) {
                        /**
                         * This will handle for customers.
                         */
                        if (this.isCustomer) {
                            this.$axios.post('https://yumi.market/api/compare-items', {
                                    'product_id': productId
                                })
                                .then(response => {
                                    this.$emitter.emit('add-flash', { type: 'success', message: response.data.data.message });
                                })
                                .catch(error => {
                                    if ([400, 422].includes(error.response.status)) {
                                        this.$emitter.emit('add-flash', { type: 'warning', message: error.response.data.data.message });

                                        return;
                                    }

                                    this.$emitter.emit('add-flash', { type: 'error', message: error.response.data.message});
                                });

                            return;
                        }

                        /**
                         * This will handle for guests.
                         */
                        let existingItems = this.getStorageValue(this.getCompareItemsStorageKey()) ?? [];

                        if (existingItems.length) {
                            if (! existingItems.includes(productId)) {
                                existingItems.push(productId);

                                this.setStorageValue(this.getCompareItemsStorageKey(), existingItems);

                                this.$emitter.emit('add-flash', { type: 'success', message: "Товар додано в порівняння." });
                            } else {
                                this.$emitter.emit('add-flash', { type: 'warning', message: "Товар вже додано в порівняння." });
                            }
                        } else {
                            this.setStorageValue(this.getCompareItemsStorageKey(), [productId]);

                            this.$emitter.emit('add-flash', { type: 'success', message: "Товар додано в порівняння." });
                        }
                    },

                    updateQty(quantity, id) {
                        this.isLoading = true;

                        let qty = {};

                        qty[id] = quantity;

                        this.$axios.put('https://yumi.market/api/checkout/cart', { qty })
                            .then(response => {
                                if (response.data.message) {
                                    this.cart = response.data.data;
                                } else {
                                    this.$emitter.emit('add-flash', { type: 'warning', message: response.data.data.message });
                                }

                                this.isLoading = false;
                            }).catch(error => this.isLoading = false);
                    },

                    getCompareItemsStorageKey() {
                        return 'compare_items';
                    },

                    setStorageValue(key, value) {
                        localStorage.setItem(key, JSON.stringify(value));
                    },

                    getStorageValue(key) {
                        let value = localStorage.getItem(key);

                        if (value) {
                            value = JSON.parse(value);
                        }

                        return value;
                    },

                    scrollToReview() {
                        let accordianElement = document.querySelector('#review-accordian-button');

                        if (accordianElement) {
                            accordianElement.click();

                            accordianElement.scrollIntoView({
                                behavior: 'smooth'
                            });
                        }

                        let tabElement = document.querySelector('#review-tab-button');

                        if (tabElement) {
                            tabElement.click();

                            tabElement.scrollIntoView({
                                behavior: 'smooth'
                            });
                        }
                    },

                    ensureQuantity(formData) {
                        if (! formData.has('quantity')) {
                            formData.append('quantity', 1);
                        }
                    },

                    submitStockAlert() {
                        if (this.isStoring.stockAlert) {
                            return;
                        }

                        this.isStoring.stockAlert = true;

                        this.$axios.post('https://yumi.market/api/products/220/stock-alert', {
                                contact: this.stockAlertContact,
                            })
                            .then(response => {
                                this.$emitter.emit('add-flash', {
                                    type: 'success',
                                    message: response.data.message,
                                });
                            })
                            .catch(error => {
                                this.$emitter.emit('add-flash', {
                                    type: 'warning',
                                    message: error.response?.data?.message || "\u041d\u0435 \u0432\u0434\u0430\u043b\u043e\u0441\u044f \u0437\u0431\u0435\u0440\u0435\u0433\u0442\u0438 \u0437\u0430\u044f\u0432\u043a\u0443. \u0421\u043f\u0440\u043e\u0431\u0443\u0439\u0442\u0435 \u0449\u0435 \u0440\u0430\u0437.",
                                });
                            })
                            .finally(() => {
                                this.isStoring.stockAlert = false;
                            });
                    },

                    copyProductLink() {
    const url = new URL(window.location.href);

    url.searchParams.set('locale', "uk");
    url.searchParams.set('currency', "UAH");

    const link = url.toString();

    if (navigator.clipboard && window.isSecureContext) {
        navigator.clipboard.writeText(link)
            .then(() => this.onProductLinkCopied())
            .catch(() => this.copyProductLinkFallback(link));

        return;
    }

    this.copyProductLinkFallback(link);
},

copyProductLinkFallback(link) {
    const textarea = document.createElement('textarea');

    textarea.value = link;
    textarea.setAttribute('readonly', '');
    textarea.style.position = 'fixed';
    textarea.style.top = '-9999px';
    textarea.style.left = '-9999px';

    document.body.appendChild(textarea);

    textarea.focus();
    textarea.select();
    textarea.setSelectionRange(0, textarea.value.length);

    let copied = false;

    try {
        copied = document.execCommand('copy');
    } catch (error) {
        copied = false;
    }

    document.body.removeChild(textarea);

    if (copied) {
        this.onProductLinkCopied();

        return;
    }

    window.prompt(this.shareTranslations.copy, link);

    this.$emitter.emit('add-flash', {
        type: 'warning',
        message: this.shareTranslations.failed
    });
},

onProductLinkCopied() {
    this.copiedLink = true;

    this.$emitter.emit('add-flash', {
        type: 'success',
        message: this.shareTranslations.copied
    });

    setTimeout(() => {
        this.copiedLink = false;
    }, 2000);
},
                },
            });
        </script>
        <script
        type="text/x-template"
        id="v-mini-cart-toggle-template"
    >
        <button
            type="button"
            class="relative inline-flex h-7 w-7 items-center justify-center"
            aria-label="Кошик"
            @click="$emitter.emit('open-mini-cart')"
        >
            <span
                class="icon-cart text-2xl"
                aria-hidden="true"
            ></span>

                            <span
                    v-if="cart?.items_count"
                    class="yumi-header-counter ltr:left-5 rtl:right-5 max-md:ltr:left-6 max-md:rtl:right-4"
                >
                    {{ cart.items_count }}
                </span>
                    </button>
    </script>

    <script type="module">
        app.component('v-mini-cart-toggle', {
            template: '#v-mini-cart-toggle-template',

            data() {
                return {
                    cart: null,
                };
            },

            mounted() {
                this.$emitter.on('update-mini-cart', this.updateCart);
            },

            beforeUnmount() {
                this.$emitter.off('update-mini-cart', this.updateCart);
            },

            methods: {
                updateCart(cart) {
                    this.cart = cart;
                },
            },
        });
    </script>
    <script type="text/x-template" id="v-header-wishlist-template">
        <a
            href="https://yumi.market/customer/account/wishlist"
            class="inline-flex h-7 w-7 items-center justify-center"
            aria-label="Список бажань"
        >
            <span class="icon-heart text-2xl" aria-hidden="true"></span>

            <span
                v-if="count"
                class="yumi-header-counter ltr:left-5 rtl:right-5 max-md:ltr:left-6 max-md:rtl:right-4"
            >
                {{ count }}
            </span>
        </a>
    </script>

    <script type="module">
        app.component('v-header-wishlist', {
            template: '#v-header-wishlist-template',

            props: {
                initialCount: {
                    type: Number,
                    default: 0,
                },

                isCustomer: {
                    type: Boolean,
                    default: false,
                },
            },

            data() {
                return {
                    count: this.initialCount,
                    syncVersion: 0,
                };
            },

            mounted() {
                this.$emitter.on('update-wishlist', this.updateCount);

                if (this.isCustomer) {
                    this.fetchCount();
                }
            },

            beforeUnmount() {
                this.$emitter.off('update-wishlist', this.updateCount);
            },

            methods: {
                updateCount(items) {
                    if (Array.isArray(items)) {
                        this.syncVersion++;
                        this.count = items.length;

                        return;
                    }

                    if (Number.isInteger(items?.delta)) {
                        this.syncVersion++;
                        this.count = Math.max(0, this.count + items.delta);

                        return;
                    }

                    if (this.isCustomer) {
                        this.fetchCount();
                    }
                },

                fetchCount() {
                    const version = ++this.syncVersion;

                    if (! window.yumiWishlistCountPromise) {
                        const request = this.$axios.get('https://yumi.market/api/customer/wishlist');

                        window.yumiWishlistCountPromise = request;

                        const clearRequest = () => {
                            if (window.yumiWishlistCountPromise === request) {
                                window.yumiWishlistCountPromise = null;
                            }
                        };

                        request.then(clearRequest, clearRequest);
                    }

                    window.yumiWishlistCountPromise
                        .then(response => {
                            if (version !== this.syncVersion) {
                                return;
                            }

                            const items = response.data?.data;

                            this.count = Array.isArray(items) ? items.length : 0;
                        })
                        .catch(() => {});
                },
            },
        });
    </script>
  <script type="text/x-template" id="v-main-menu-dropdown-template">
    <div ref="root">
        <!-- TOGGLE -->
        <button
            type="button"
            class="select-none"
            @click.stop="toggle"
            @focus="$emit('prefetch')"
            @mouseenter="$emit('prefetch')"
            :aria-expanded="isActive ? 'true' : 'false'"
        >
            <slot name="toggle">Toggle</slot>
        </button>

        <!-- Overlay -->
        <transition
            name="main-menu-overlay"
            enter-active-class="transition-opacity duration-300 ease-out"
            enter-from-class="opacity-0"
            enter-to-class="opacity-100"
            leave-active-class="transition-opacity duration-200 ease-in"
            leave-from-class="opacity-100"
            leave-to-class="opacity-0"
        >
            <div
                v-show="isActive"
                data-main-menu-overlay
                class="fixed inset-x-0 bottom-0 z-20 bg-gray-500 bg-opacity-50"
                :style="{ top: `${dropdownTop}px` }"
                aria-hidden="true"
                @click="close"
            ></div>
        </transition>

        <!-- DROPDOWN -->
        <transition
            name="dropdown"
            enter-active-class="transition duration-150 ease-out"
            enter-from-class="opacity-0 translate-y-2"
            enter-to-class="opacity-100 translate-y-0"
            leave-active-class="transition duration-100 ease-in"
            leave-from-class="opacity-100 translate-y-0"
            leave-to-class="opacity-0 translate-y-2"
        >
            <div
                v-show="isActive"
                data-main-menu-content
                class="main-menu-dynamic-top fixed left-0 right-0 z-30 bg-white shadow-xl"
                :style="{ top: `${dropdownTop}px` }"
            >
                <!-- FULL WIDTH CONTENT -->
                <slot name="content"></slot>
                <slot name="menu"></slot>
            </div>
        </transition>
    </div>
</script>




    <script type="module">
       app.component('v-main-menu-dropdown', {
            template: '#v-main-menu-dropdown-template',

            emits: ['toggle', 'prefetch'],

       data() {
            return {
                isActive: false,
                dropdownTop: 0,
                lockedScrollX: 0,
                lockedScrollY: 0,
            };
    },
        beforeUnmount() {
                this.detachOpenStateListeners();
            },


        methods: {
        updateDropdownTop() {
            const header = document.querySelector('header');

            if (! header) {
                this.dropdownTop = 0;
                return;
            }

            this.dropdownTop = Math.max(0, Math.round(header.getBoundingClientRect().bottom));
        },

        attachOpenStateListeners() {
            document.addEventListener('click', this.onClickOutside);
            document.addEventListener('keydown', this.onKeydown);
            document.addEventListener('wheel', this.onWheel, { passive: false });
            document.addEventListener('touchmove', this.onTouchMove, { passive: false });
            window.addEventListener('resize', this.updateDropdownTop, { passive: true });
            window.addEventListener('scroll', this.restoreLockedScroll, { passive: true });
        },

        detachOpenStateListeners() {
            document.removeEventListener('click', this.onClickOutside);
            document.removeEventListener('keydown', this.onKeydown);
            document.removeEventListener('wheel', this.onWheel);
            document.removeEventListener('touchmove', this.onTouchMove);
            window.removeEventListener('resize', this.updateDropdownTop);
            window.removeEventListener('scroll', this.restoreLockedScroll);
        },

        toggle() {
            if (this.isActive) {
                this.close();
                return;
            }

            this.open();
        },

        open() {
            this.updateDropdownTop();
            this.lockedScrollX = window.scrollX;
            this.lockedScrollY = window.scrollY;
            this.isActive = true;
            this.attachOpenStateListeners();
            this.$emit('toggle', { isActive: true });
        },

        close() {
            if (! this.isActive) {
                return;
            }

            this.isActive = false;
            this.detachOpenStateListeners();
            this.$emit('toggle', { isActive: false });
        },

        restoreLockedScroll() {
            if (
                window.scrollX === this.lockedScrollX
                && window.scrollY === this.lockedScrollY
            ) {
                return;
            }

            window.scrollTo(this.lockedScrollX, this.lockedScrollY);
        },

        canScrollMenuElement(target, deltaY) {
            if (! (target instanceof Element)) {
                return false;
            }

            const menu = target.closest('[data-main-menu-content]');

            if (! menu) {
                return false;
            }

            let element = target;

            while (element && menu.contains(element)) {
                const style = window.getComputedStyle(element);
                const isScrollable = ['auto', 'scroll'].includes(style.overflowY)
                    && element.scrollHeight > element.clientHeight;

                if (
                    isScrollable
                    && (
                        (deltaY < 0 && element.scrollTop > 0)
                        || (deltaY > 0 && element.scrollTop + element.clientHeight < element.scrollHeight)
                    )
                ) {
                    return true;
                }

                if (element === menu) {
                    break;
                }

                element = element.parentElement;
            }

            return false;
        },

        onWheel(event) {
            if (! this.canScrollMenuElement(event.target, event.deltaY)) {
                event.preventDefault();
            }
        },

        onTouchMove(event) {
            if (! (event.target instanceof Element)
                || ! event.target.closest('[data-main-menu-content]')
            ) {
                event.preventDefault();
            }
        },

        onClickOutside(e) {
            if (! this.isActive) {
                return;
            }

            const root = this.$refs.root;

            if (root && !root.contains(e.target)) {
                this.close();
            }
        },

        onKeydown(event) {
            if (event.key === 'Escape') {
                this.close();
            }
        },
    },
});
    </script>


<script>
    /**
     * Mount after the document has been parsed and all deferred module
     * scripts have registered their Vue components.
     */
    window.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", function (event) {
        app.mount("#app");
    });
</script>
